Motivation: 精确等变架构通常将规定的群作用编码到专用算子中，这阻碍了其与通用骨干网络的重用以及跨数据模态的迁移，亟需一种更灵活、可移植的表示级等变设计原则。

Method: 提出GARI表示接口：构建生成器索引流，转换为通用交互帧，共享参数处理，修复排序导致的上下文失配，允许跨流信息交换，并通过流间差异进行聚合。引入探针特定软等变残差和直接等变误差（DEE）作为诊断指标。

Result: 在基因组序列、图像和三维点云等不同模态上，针对序列逆转、平面旋转反射和轴向转移等变换，GARI均支持任务相关的变换一致性和对保留探针的泛化，无需对序列主干进行群特定的重新设计。

Conclusion: GARI为硬等变架构提供了可移植的诊断补充：使生成器结构可访问、可学习、可测量，同时有限探针证据与连续群上精确等变的认证保持区别。

Abstract: Exact-equivariant architectures typically encode prescribed group actions in specialized operators, which can complicate their reuse with generic backbones and across data modalities. We introduce the Generator-Aligned Representation Interface (GARI), a representation-level design principle that exposes selected transformation generators to a generic sequence backbone through aligned canonical and generator-induced views. We formalize the resulting behavior using a probe-specific soft-equivariance residual defined over declared data and transformation distributions. This framework distinguishes representation consistency from task robustness and exact equivariance, and localizes residual mismatch to interface construction, shared stream processing, and terminal fusion. We instantiate the interface as GARI-Net, which constructs generator-indexed streams, converts them into a common interaction frame, processes them with shared parameters, repairs ordering-induced context mismatch, enables cross-stream information exchange, and aggregates them using inter-stream discrepancy. Direct Equivariance Error (DEE) provides a frozen-checkpoint diagnostic of the prescribed representation relation under known token or voxel actions. Experiments on genomic sequences, images, and three-dimensional point clouds examine sequence reversal, planar rotations and reflections, and controlled axial transfer. Across these settings, the same interface principle supports task-relevant transformation consistency and generalization to declared held-out probes without requiring group-specific redesign of the sequence backbone. GARI therefore provides a portable diagnostic complement to hard-equivariant architectures: it makes generator structure accessible, learnable, and measurable, while finite-probe evidence remains distinct from certification of exact equivariance over a continuous group.

</details>


### [74] [Empirical Evaluation of Out-Of-Distribution Performance of Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26000)
*Malena Loza,David Chushig-Muzo,Eva Milara,Luis Bote-Curiel,Luis Estrada-Petrocelli,Felipe Grijalva*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了9种表格基础模型在分布漂移下的鲁棒性，发现所有模型均出现性能退化，漂移差距在0.003-0.060之间，且存在可扩展性瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格基础模型大多在独立同分布假设下训练和评估，但现实场景中存在分布漂移，而针对表格基础模型在分布漂移下的研究非常有限。

Method: 选取9种涵盖不同预训练策略和架构的TFMs（TabPFN系列、TabICL系列、Mitra、LimiX、TabFM），使用TableShift研究中的3个真实数据集（HELOC、Voting、Childhood Lead），覆盖标签、社会经济和地理三种漂移类型，进行全面的OOD性能评估。

Result: 所有TFMs在分布漂移下均出现系统性性能退化，漂移差距因漂移类型不同在0.003到0.060之间；ID与OOD性能之间的关系延续了经典表格模型的规律；高性能模型存在显著的可扩展性瓶颈，资源需求超出标准部署基础设施。

Conclusion: 该研究扩展了表格数据OOD基准，证明了TFMs在结构分布漂移的高风险领域中仍需谨慎采用，且当前TFMs在鲁棒性和可扩展性方面均存在不足。

Abstract: Tabular Foundation Models (TFMs) have emerged as novel approaches for tabular predictive tasks, demonstrating competitive predictive performance to ensemble tree-based models. Most TFMs are trained and evaluated on independent and identically distributed data, but this assumption changes in real-world scenarios due to distribution shifts, which compromise the robustness of models. Limited research has been conducted of TFMs under distribution shifts. We present an empirical evaluation of Out-Of-Distribution (OOD) performance of nine TFMs, spanning diverse pre-training strategies and architectures: TabPFNv2, TabPFNv2.5, TabPFNv2.6, TabPFNv3, TabICL, TabICLv2, Mitra, LimiX and TabFM. Three real-world datasets from the TableShift study were considered (HELOC, Voting, Childhood Lead), covering label, socioeconomic, and geographic shift types. Our results show that all evaluated TFMs degrade systematically under distribution shift regardless of pre-training strategy, with shift gaps ranging from 0.003 to 0.060 depending on shift type. The relationship between in-distribution and OOD predictive performance documented for classical tabular models extends into TFMs. We also identified a scalability gap, as high-performing models demand significant memory and computational resources beyond what standard deployment infrastructure can support. This study extends existing benchmarks for OOD in tabular data, providing evidence to support their adoption in high-stakes domains characterized by structural distribution shifts.

</details>


### [75] [Re-thinking Mammography Transfer Learning: The Dataset-Informed Transfer Learning (DITL) Framework for Breast Cancer Screening and Lesion Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26043)
*Adarsh Bhandary Panambur,Siming Bayer,Andreas Maier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DITL框架，通过自适应难度加权交叉熵(A-DWCE)和自适应邻域表示三元组损失(A-NR-Triplet)两个组件，在无需超参数调优的情况下，在乳腺钼靶X线摄影分类任务上实现了对大规模数据集和小型ROI数据集均显著的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统迁移学习忽略数据集特定特征，而现有邻域信息方法受限于狭窄任务和僵化公式，无法扩展到大规模人群级数据集。

Method: 提出DITL框架，包含两个自适应组件：(i) A-DWCE：基于自监督特征空间中k近邻标签纯度分配每样本权重；(ii) A-NR-Triplet：使用可学习边距增强类内紧凑性和类间分离性。无需超参数调优，计算开销可忽略。

Result: 在VinDR-Mammo大规模数据集上，乳腺密度分类的准确率、F1分数和AUC均达SOTA水平(p<0.0001)；在小型ROI数据集上也获得一致且统计显著的提升(p<0.0001)。

Conclusion: DITL建立了从筛查到诊断的全谱系乳腺钼靶分类框架，兼具临床相关性、可扩展性和泛化性，桥接了小型病灶分析与大规模密度估计。

Abstract: Enhancing classification performance in mammography remains a persistent challenge across both small curated datasets and large-scale clinical cohorts. Conventional transfer learning approaches often neglect dataset-specific characteristics, while recent neighborhood-informed methods have been restricted to narrow tasks with rigid formulations, limiting their scalability to population-level datasets. To address these challenges, we propose the Dataset-Informed Transfer Learning (DITL) framework, which integrates dataset-derived difficulty signals with neighborhood-based triplet supervision in a unified objective. DITL introduces two adaptive components: (i) Adaptive Difficulty-Weighted Cross-Entropy (A-DWCE), which assigns per-sample weights based on k-nearest neighbor label purity in a self-supervised feature space, and (ii) Adaptive Neighborhood Representation Triplet (A-NR-Triplet), which enforces intra-class compactness and inter-class separation using a learnable margin. Unlike focal loss, DITL requires no hyperparameter tuning, removes heuristic weighting and fixed margins, and incurs negligible computational overhead, yielding a robust and scalable optimization strategy. On the large-scale VinDR-Mammo dataset, DITL achieves state-of-the-art performance for whole-image breast density classification, with significant improvements across accuracy, F1-score, and AUC (p < 0.0001). Beyond large cohorts, DITL also delivers consistent, statistically significant gains on small ROI datasets (p < 0.0001). By bridging small-scale lesion analysis with large-scale density estimation, DITL establishes a clinically relevant, scalable, and generalizable framework for mammography classification, spanning the full breast cancer screening-to-diagnosis spectrum.

</details>


### [76] [Spend Experts Where You Are Unsure: Confidence-Adaptive Routing for Mixture-of-Experts LoRA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26052)
*Tom Saliencro,Rohan Desai,Priya Nair,Maya Lindqvist,Daniel Whitmore*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CARE是一种基于置信度自适应路由的MoE-LoRA方法，根据token的不确定性动态调整激活的专家数量，在保持性能的同时减少计算开销，并提升OOD检测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MoE-LoRA对每个token固定激活k个专家，但不同token的模型不确定性不同，固定k值导致简单token过度计算、困难token计算不足。作者希望利用路由器输出的分布作为不确定性信号，实现自适应专家激活。

Method: 提出CARE方法：采用核采样方式激活专家，按路由器权重降序激活直到累积质量达到阈值；当已激活专家存在分歧时进行少量扩展；引入预算恒温器校准阈值以匹配目标平均激活数。该方法无需额外参数，可单次前向传播完成。

Result: 在LLaMA-3.1-8B和Qwen2.5-7B上的8个常识推理基准、数学、代码和知识任务中，CARE在匹配计算量的情况下优于固定top-k MoE-LoRA；以更少专家激活达到固定k=4基线的水平；同时置信度和分歧信号在OOD检测上优于MSP、熵和多pass代理方法。

Conclusion: CARE通过核采样和分歧检测实现了高效的动态专家路由，具有核保真度、预算最优性等理论支持，是一种即插即用、无额外参数的计算高效方案。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) variants of Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) route every token to a fixed number of experts $k$. Tokens differ in how uncertain the model is about them, so a single k over-spends on easy tokens and under-serves hard ones. We observe that the router's output distribution is already a per-token uncertainty signal: peaked mass indicates confidence, while a flat distribution indicates ambiguity. We introduce CARE (Confidence-Adaptive Routing of Experts), which admits experts in a nucleus fashion. Experts are activated in decreasing router weight until their cumulative mass reaches a threshold, with a small extension when the admitted experts disagree. A budget thermostat calibrates the threshold so that the average number of active experts matches any target. CARE is a drop-in, single-forward-pass rule with no extra parameters. Across eight commonsense benchmarks on LLaMA-3.1-8B and Qwen2.5-7B, as well as math, code, and knowledge tasks, CARE improves over fixed top-k MoE-LoRA at matched compute and matches the fixed-k=4 baseline while activating fewer experts. The same confidence and disagreement signals also improve out-of-distribution detection over MSP, entropy, and multi-pass proxies. We support the design with nucleus fidelity, budget optimality, and an epistemic reading of disagreement, and we release code.

</details>


### [77] [Domain-Prior-Regularized Graph Modeling for Anomaly Detection in Cyber-Physical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23197)
*Youngseok Hwang,Joonsung Kwon,Geonwoo Lee,Hyunwoo Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DPR-GM是一种利用大语言模型从系统文档中提取物理耦合关系作为结构先验的图建模框架，通过领域先验正则化在数据稀缺的小规模CPS中实现优于现有方法的异常检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于图的多元传感器时间序列异常检测方法在小规模物理系统中面临标注异常稀缺、正常数据有限的问题，容易捕获虚假相关并产生不稳定的传感器拓扑结构。

Method: 利用大语言模型从系统文档提取传感器间的有向物理耦合关系，编码为二值领域邻接矩阵作为结构门控；结合正常训练数据的皮尔逊相关进行调制；异常分数进一步通过变异系数计算的传感器级可靠性加权。所有图组件在训练前固定，不引入可学习参数。

Result: 在SKAB基准上，DPR-GM在F1、AUROC和AUPRC指标上均优于基于图、统计和深度学习的基线方法。

Conclusion: 领域结构化图先验在数据稀缺的CPS场景中是可完全学习的拓扑结构的实用替代方案。

Abstract: Anomaly detection on multivariate sensor time series is critical for industrial monitoring of cyber-physical systems (CPS), where even subtle deviations from normal behavior can indicate process disruption. Recent graph-based approaches have made significant progress, but they often struggle in small-scale physical systems with scarce labeled anomalies and limited normal data. In such settings, graph-based models tend to capture spurious correlations and produce unstable sensor topologies. We propose DPR-GM (Domain-Prior-Regularized Graph Modeling), a forecasting-based framework that incorporates system design knowledge into graph construction. DPR-GM leverages a large language model (LLM) to extract directed physical couplings between sensor pairs from system documentation, which are encoded as a binary domain adjacency matrix serving as a structural gate over sensor relations. This gate is then modulated by Pearson correlations estimated from normal training data. The anomaly score is further weighted by sensor-level reliability derived from the coefficient of variation. All graph and weighting components are fixed prior to training and add no learnable parameters. On the SKAB benchmark, DPR-GM outperforms graph-based, statistical, and deep learning baselines across F1, AUROC, and AUPRC, showing that domain-structured graph priors are a practical alternative to fully learned topologies in data-scarce CPS.

</details>

### [78] [Lloyd's $K$-Means Clustering Algorithm Is Frank-Wolfe in Disguise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25190)
*Michael Pokojovy,J. Marcus Jobe,Simon Lacoste-Julien*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文建立了Lloyd's K-means算法与Frank-Wolfe算法之间的理论联系，证明Lloyd算法是FW的一个特例，并利用FW在凹目标上的最新进展，推导出SSE目标函数达到局部最小值的非渐近O(1/t)收敛率。针对空簇问题，还开发了适用于半光滑目标的FW变体，保持相同的收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管Lloyd's K-means被广泛使用，但其收敛性质的理论理解仍不完善。作者希望通过建立与Frank-Wolfe算法的联系，借助FW成熟的理论框架来刻画K-means的收敛行为。

Method: 建立Lloyd算法与Frank-Wolfe算法之间的等价关系，将K-means的SSE最小化问题重新表述为FW框架下的优化问题；针对空簇问题，提出适用于半光滑目标的FW变体；通过球形高斯混合模拟和真实图像分割数据集进行实验验证。

Result: 证明了Lloyd算法是FW算法的特例，获得了非渐近O(1/t)的收敛率到SSE局部最小值，该收敛率仅由初始SSE值控制。

Conclusion: 这项工作为K-means算法提供了新的理论视角，通过FW框架统一了对K-means收敛性的理解，并为处理空簇等实际问题提供了理论保证。

Abstract: Lloyd's $K$-means algorithm, also known as naïve $K$-means, is a widely used ad hoc optimization heuristic, designed to minimize the sum of squared errors (SSE) across all $K$-partitions of a dataset via iterative cluster refinement. In this work, we establish a novel connection between Lloyd's algorithm and the Frank-Wolfe (FW) algorithm, a prominent first-order method for projection-free optimization. We demonstrate that Lloyd's algorithm is a special case of FW. Leveraging recent advances in FW methods for concave objectives, we derive a non-asymptotic $\mathcal{O}(1/t)$ convergence rate to a local minimum of the SSE objective. To account for empty clusters, an outcome possible under Lloyd's greedy assignment, we develop an FW variant for semismooth objectives while retaining the same convergence rate that is solely controlled by the initial SSE value. We illustrate our findings with a simulation study for spherical Gaussian mixtures and a real-world image segmentation dataset.

</details>


### [79] [Learning from the Unseen: Offline Reinforcement Learning with Hidden Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25241)
*Zeyu Bian,Ying Zhou,Yifan Cui*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文首次研究了离线强化学习中动作变量存在隐藏（仅观测到带噪声的代理变量）的问题，提出了基于影响函数的多重稳健估计器LURE，利用下一状态作为未观测动作的自然代理，实现了策略价值的识别、一致估计和有效统计推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准离线强化学习算法假设数据集中的动作被无误差观测，但许多实际应用中真实动作不可观测，只能获得带噪声的代理变量，导致现有方法产生有偏甚至误导性的结论。

Method: 利用下一状态变量作为未观测动作的自然代理，建立策略价值的可识别性；提出基于影响函数的估计器LURE；该估计器具有多重稳健性，在多种组合的 nuisance 成分正确设定下保持一致性；且具有渐近正态性，支持有效的统计推断。

Result: 通过仿真实验和基于MIMIC-III数据库的脓毒症管理应用验证了LURE的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文是首个解决含隐藏动作的离线强化学习问题的工作，LURE在理论性质和实际性能上均表现良好。

Abstract: Standard offline reinforcement learning (RL) algorithms typically assume that the actions in the dataset are observed without error. However, in many real-world applications, the true actions are unobserved and only noisy proxies are available, causing existing RL methods to yield biased and potentially misleading conclusions. We study off-policy evaluation in infinite-horizon discounted Markov decision processes with hidden actions. By leveraging the next-state variable as a natural proxy for the unobserved action, we establish identification of the policy value and propose an influence-function-based estimator called LURE (Learning from the Unseen: Robust Estimator). LURE is multiply robust, remaining consistent under several combinations of correctly specified nuisance components, and is asymptotically normal, enabling valid statistical inference. To our knowledge, this is the first work to address offline RL with hidden actions. We demonstrate LURE's effectiveness through simulations and a sepsis management application using the MIMIC-III database.

</details>


### [80] [Can Deep Generative Models Reproduce Non-Stationary Gaussian Random Fields?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25929)
*Daniel Kua,Yan Song*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文评估了四种深度生成模型（FM、DDPM、score-SDE、VAE）在已知非平稳高斯随机场上的表现，发现所有模型都能恢复均值表面，但在协方差恢复上存在显著差异：DDPM和score-SDE表现较好，FM略有衰减，VAE难以恢复协方差结构。ERA5温度异常实验进一步验证了该评估框架的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度生成模型广泛应用于空间和时空建模，但评估其是否真正学习了底层数据生成过程十分困难，因为真实情况未知且评估通常仅依赖观测数据。本文旨在建立系统的评估框架，量化DGM对真实空间结构的恢复能力。

Method: 选取四种代表性深度生成模型（流匹配FM、去噪扩散概率模型DDPM、得分SDE、变分自编码器VAE），在已知的非平稳高斯随机场上进行实验，利用oracle样本和平稳对照作为参考，通过全面的度量指标评估模型对真实均值和协方差结构的恢复情况，并在ERA5温度异常数据上进一步验证。

Result: 四种模型均能较好地恢复均值表面；协方差恢复方面，DDPM和score-SDE表现合理，FM的非平稳性略有衰减且方差略微欠分散，VAE则难以恢复协方差结构。

Conclusion: 所提出的评估框架能够有效支持复杂真实世界时空数据上DGM的验证与开发，并揭示了不同生成模型家族在协方差结构学习上的本质差异。

Abstract: Deep generative models (DGMs) are widely used for complex high-dimensional data and increasingly applied to spatial and spatio-temporal modeling. Their generated samples implicitly represent the learned data distribution and associated uncertainty. However, for real-world data, assessing whether DGMs have learned the underlying process is difficult because the ground truth is unknown and evaluation often relies on observations alone. We evaluate representative DGMs, flow matching (FM), DDPM, score-SDE, and VAE, on a known non-stationary Gaussian random field. This paper provides comprehensive metrics to assess recovery of the ground-truth mean and covariance structures, with oracle samples and a stationary control as references. All four models recover the mean surface, while their covariance recovery differs across model families: DDPM and score-SDE recover the covariance structure reasonably well, FM exhibits mildly attenuated non-stationarity and slight variance under-dispersion, and VAE has difficulty recovering the covariance structure. An experiment on ERA5 temperature anomalies further demonstrates how the framework can support the validation and development of DGMs for complex real-world spatio-temporal data.

</details>

### [81] [Parallel Model-Based Derivative-Free Optimization via Rank-Two KKT Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24813)
*Donghan Wu,Pengcheng Xie*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于反射操作的模型更新策略，通过Sherman-Morrison-Woodbury公式将模型拟合的计算成本从O(m³)降低至O(n²)，并保持了插值集的平衡常数和信任域等距性质，首次在模型类信任域DFO方法中实现了一阶全局收敛保证下的并行可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于欠定二次插值模型的信任域方法在求解KKT系统更新模型参数时需要O(m³)计算，这严重限制了并行可扩展性。本文旨在降低模型更新的计算复杂度，同时保证算法的收敛性质。

Method: 利用最小Frobenius范数更新模型中KKT矩阵的结构特性——其完全依赖于位移坐标的内积。通过沿单个坐标轴反射插值集，可以保持这些内积不变，仅改变KKT矩阵的一行和一列，形成秩至多为2的扰动，从而利用Sherman-Morrison-Woodbury公式以O(n²)代价更新逆矩阵。该反射操作在中心化的欧几里得信任域中是等距变换，且不破坏插值集的平衡常数（poisedness constant）。将此机制嵌入主从并行算法中，并使用截断共轭梯度法求解子问题。

Result: 在530个基准问题上进行了数值实验，与已有的DFO求解器进行了性能对比。

Conclusion: 所提出的反射更新机制在保持标准全线性模型管理假设的前提下，首次在模型类信任域DFO方法中实现了O(n²)的模型更新成本和一阶全局收敛保证，显著提升了并行可扩展性。

Abstract: Derivative-free optimization (DFO) addresses unconstrained problems $\min_{\x\in\RR^n} f(\x)$ where $f$ is accessed only through a zeroth-order oracle. Model-based trust-region methods construct underdetermined quadratic interpolation models from $\mathcal{O}(n)$ points and solve a KKT system to determine model parameters, costing $\mathcal{O}(m^3)$ operations and limiting parallel scalability. It is shown that the KKT matrix for the minimum Frobenius norm updating model depends entirely on inner products of shifted coordinates. Reflecting the interpolation set across a single coordinate axis preserves these inner products and changes only one row and column of the KKT matrix, inducing a rank-at-most-two perturbation whose inverse update via the Sherman-Morrison-Woodbury formula costs $\mathcal{O}(n^2)$ when $m=\mathcal{O}(n)$. The reflection is an isometry in centered Euclidean trust regions and preserves the poisedness constant of the interpolation set; together with standard fully linear model-management assumptions this supports first-order global convergence. The mechanism is embedded in a master-worker parallel algorithm with a Truncated Conjugate Gradient subproblem solver. Numerical results on 530 benchmark problems compare performance against an established DFO solver.

</details>

Abstract: This paper investigates the problem of optimal piecewise linear approximation of a smooth curve, in which the area of the region enclosed between the graph of the original function and the constructed interpolating polyline is minimized. For a cubic function, it is demonstrated that the optimal interpolation nodes are distributed non-uniformly; in the case of two internal partition points, their exact coordinates and the corresponding minimum area are obtained. Abstract: This paper develops a functional H_infinity filter for nonlinear descriptor systems subject to external disturbances. Conventional H_infinity filtering approaches for descriptor systems impose restrictive regularity assumptions and employ implicit descriptor-form filters, leading to practical implementation difficulties. Moreover, existing approaches mainly target full-or reduced-order state estimation, which is computationally inefficient when only a specific functional of the state is required. To address these limitations, the filter is formulated directly in an explicit state-space framework and can be initialized with arbitrary initial values. The filter order is chosen to be less than or equal to the dimension of the functional vector to be estimated, thereby reducing computational complexity. The considered nonlinearities are characterized using incremental quadratic constraints parameterized by appropriate multiplier matrices, which encompass Lipschitz, one-sided Lipschitz, monotone, and many other nonlinearities. Sufficient criteria for the existence of the proposed filter are established through a rank condition imposed on the system matrices together with a set of linear matrix inequalities (LMIs). Under these conditions, asymptotic stability of the estimation error dynamics is guaranteed, while the influence of external disturbances on the error is bounded within a prescribed L2-performance framework. Finally, numerical simulations demonstrate and validate the effectiveness of our theoretical results.

Abstract: A Derivative-Free Optimization (DFO) model is developed and analyzed for solving inverse structured symmetric matrix problems for which the eigenvalues are specified. Some (zero and nonzero) entries are preassigned and cannot be changed, while others should be nonzero but their values are not given. The rest of the entries are completely free. The obtained matrix must meet these criteria and have the specified eigenvalues. This specialized inverse eigenvalue problem is relevant to various applications and is linked to determining the graph, with weights on the undirected edges, of the matrix associated with its sparse pattern. Our novel optimization model requires computing the eigenvalues of a symmetric matrix to evaluate the non-differentiable objective function. We apply deterministic DFO schemes, specifically the global variant GLODS of the well-known family of directional direct search (DDS) methods. We discuss its convergence properties which are based on the fact that the objective function of our model is Lipschitz continuous. Additionally, we explore the potential benefits of using several well-established heuristic strategies to solve the proposed optimization model. We present preliminary numerical results to illustrate and compare the performance of the considered deterministic and heuristic DFO options in various possible scenarios.

Abstract: Modern power grids are increasingly vulnerable to coordinated cyber-attacks, particularly false data injection attacks (FDIAs) that can evade conventional residual-based detectors. While most existing detection methods rely on instantaneous measurements, coordinated dynamic attacks can remain stealthy at each time step while introducing structured temporal deviations. This paper develops a joint framework for modeling and detecting such attacks in multi-area power systems. A time-aggregated attack model is first formulated to capture temporal evolution and inter-area coordination. For detection, a kernel-embedded functional subspace detection (KEFSD) method is proposed, which models residual trajectories in a reproducing kernel Hilbert space (RKHS) and employs RKHS-constrained functional principal component analysis (PCA) to identify anomalous temporal patterns. Simulation results on a modified IEEE 14-bus system demonstrates the proposed method achieves improved detection performance compared to the conventional residual based 2-norm detector.

</details>

Abstract: This paper assesses the readiness of Türkiye's electric vehicle (EV) charging infrastructure to support cost-competitive intercity travel compared to internal combustion engine (ICE) vehicles. Utilizing a directed acyclic graph-based scheduling framework, charging stops are optimized for over 2 million synthetic intercity EV trips across the national highway network, evaluating 9,641 licensed charging stations. An eight-scenario case study examines the interplay between charger power (5--350 kW) and station capacity (5 and 50 plugs), supplemented by a 200-run parametric sensitivity analysis on EV range, discharge rate, charger power, and state-of-charge (SoC) operating bounds. A three-tier economic analysis isolates operational costs, total cost of ownership (TCO), and infrastructure investments. Economically, EVs demonstrate a 23--26% operational cost advantage driven by Türkiye's favorable fuel-to-electricity price ratio, maintaining a 10--12% TCO advantage despite higher purchase prices. However, full-system infrastructure savings heavily depend on station sizing, yielding positive returns at 5 plugs per station but negative returns at 50. Ultimately, vehicle discharge rate emerges as the dominant economic lever. This study provides the first countrywide, vehicle-level assessment of intercity EV charging readiness for an emerging market, offering actionable insights for policymakers on infrastructure gaps, station sizing tradeoffs, and adoption incentives.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有网络聚类研究多聚焦于顶点中心，缺乏对聚类中心可位于边上的系统性理论分析，且不同目标函数下硬/软分配方案的结构特性尚未被充分理解。

Conclusion: 这些发现为设施选址、网络设计及基于嵌入图的相似性搜索系统提供了理论指导，并推动了高效聚类算法的开发。

Abstract: This study presents a theoretical analysis of partitional clustering on networks, analyzing both hard and soft assignment schemes with different objective functions. Cluster centers are not restricted to vertices but can also be located along the edges. We examine four key models: P-Median (PMP) and Sum of Squares Clustering (SSC) under hard assignment, and Probabilistic Distance Clustering (PDC) and Fuzzy C-Means (FCM) under soft assignment. Through mathematical analysis, we uncover structural properties that differentiate these models, such as the significance of assignment bottleneck points and the role of vertex-restricted solutions in determining optimal cluster centers. Our findings reveal that, while SSC and FCM can yield optimal centers along edges, PMP and PDC inherently favor vertex placement, leading to insights into clustering behavior on networks. These insights offer new directions for designing efficient algorithms and have implications ranging from facility location and network design to clustering on the embedding graphs that power similarity search in modern retrieval systems.

</details>

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对非凸目标与非线性等式约束下的分布式优化问题，提出了一种基于反馈线性化和奇异摄动的框架，通过将共识/可行性残差调节与可行流形上的优化分离，并引入快速残差跟踪动力学实现分布式实现，在局部二次增长条件下证明了指数收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布式优化方法在处理非凸目标与非线性等式约束时面临挑战：可行性是网络耦合的，而大多数指数收敛方法依赖凸性或仿射约束。亟需一种既能处理非线性等式约束又能保持指数收敛速度的分布式方法。

Method: 提出反馈线性化-奇异摄动框架：(1) 构建理想反馈线性化动力学，显式指定输出行为，零输出动力学对应可行流形上的投影梯度流；(2) 针对理想输入依赖于状态相关的网络耦合代数方程而无法分布式实现的问题，引入快速残差跟踪动力学替代非局部代数解，得到仅使用局部和邻居信息的奇异摄动实现；(3) 对两类问题（分布式局部非线性等式约束和非线性聚合等式约束）分别处理。

Result: 在可行流形上的局部二次增长条件下，建立了理想动力学和分布式动力学的局部指数收敛性。在足够强的时间尺度分离下，分布式实现的收敛率可任意接近理想动力学。显式欧拉离散化保留了局部指数收敛性。在更强的流形正则条件下，吸引域可扩展到可行流形的管状邻域；对于仿射局部等式约束，可扩展到整个容许状态空间。

Conclusion: 所提框架有效解决了非凸目标与非线性等式约束下的分布式优化问题，在保持指数收敛速度的同时实现了分布式实现，并具有良好的离散化性质。

Abstract: Distributed optimization becomes particularly challenging when nonconvex objectives are combined with nonlinear equality constraints: feasibility is network coupled, while most existing exponentially convergent methods rely on convexity or affine constraints. This paper introduces a feedback-linearization and singular-perturbation framework for two representative problem classes involving, respectively, distributed local nonlinear equality constraints and nonlinear aggregate equality constraints. The framework separates the regulation of consensus and feasibility residuals from optimization along the feasible manifold. Specifically, an ideal feedback-linearized dynamics is constructed whose output behavior can be explicitly assigned, while its zero-output dynamics coincides with the projected gradient flow of the aggregate objective on the feasible manifold. The ideal feedback-linearizing input, however, is determined by a state-dependent, network-coupled algebraic equation and is therefore not directly implementable in a distributed manner. To overcome this obstruction, we replace the nonlocal algebraic solution with a fast residual-tracking dynamics, yielding a singular-perturbation realization that uses only local and neighboring information. Under a local quadratic-growth condition imposed only on the feasible manifold, we establish local exponential convergence of both the ideal and distributed dynamics. For sufficiently strong time-scale separation, the convergence rate of the distributed realization can be chosen arbitrarily close to that of the ideal dynamics. Explicit Euler discretizations are also proved to preserve local exponential convergence. Under stronger manifold regularity conditions, the region of attraction extends to a tubular neighborhood of the feasible manifold and, for affine local equality constraints, to the whole admissible state space.

</details>


### [89] [Projection and contraction methods with double inertial steps for variational inclusion problems on Hilbert spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25203)
*Moin Uddin,Mohammed Alshahrani,Qamrul Hasan Ansari*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文在希尔伯特空间框架下，提出了三种结合双惯性技术的投影-收缩算法用于求解变分包含问题：算法O在单调性和Lipschitz连续性条件下弱收敛，具有自适应步长规则；算法T在强单调性假设下达到R-线性收敛；算法DIM无需强单调性即可强收敛到最小范数解。通过抽象变分包含问题、分裂可行问题和弹性网正则化问题的数值实验验证了算法性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变分包含问题在优化、博弈论和图像处理等领域有广泛应用，现有投影-收缩算法通常收敛速度有限或需要已知Lipschitz常数。本文旨在通过引入双惯性技术，在保持算法收敛性的同时提高收敛速度，并设计无需先验知识的自适应步长规则。

Method: 将双惯性技术（double inertial technique）融入投影-收缩（projection-contraction）框架，构造三种算法：算法O（自适应步长+弱收敛）、算法T（强单调性+R-线性收敛）、算法DIM（强收敛到最小范数解）。采用自适应步长规则避免对Lipschitz常数的依赖。

Result: 算法O在单调性和Lipschitz连续性下弱收敛；算法T在强单调性下达到R-线性收敛；算法DIM无需强单调性即可强收敛到最小范数解。数值实验在分裂可行问题和弹性网正则化问题上验证了算法有效性，并与现有方法进行了比较。

Conclusion: 双惯性投影-收缩算法在变分包含问题求解中具有理论优势，能够在不同假设条件下实现弱收敛、R-线性收敛或强收敛，且自适应步长设计降低了算法使用门槛。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose three projection--contraction algorithms for solving variational inclusion problems in the setting of Hilbert spaces, each incorporating a double inertial technique into the projection--contraction framework. The first algorithm \algoO achieves weak convergence under monotonicity and Lipschitz continuity of the single-valued operator, with an adaptive stepsize rule that does not require prior knowledge of the Lipschitz constant. A modified variant \algoT of \algoO attains $R$-linear convergence under the strong monotonicity assumption. The third algorithm \algoDIM obtains strong convergence to the minimum-norm solution without requiring strong monotonicity. We illustrate the proposed methods on an abstract variational inclusion problem and apply them to the split feasibility problem and the elastic net regularization problem, comparing them with existing methods in the literature. The $R$-linear convergence of \algoT is also verified through numerical simulations.

</details>


### [90] [Event-Triggered Discrete-Time Multivariable Extremum Seeking Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25223)

Abstract: This paper introduces a discrete-time event-triggered extremum seeking framework for real-time optimization of multivariable nonlinear systems. In contrast to conventional discrete-time extremum seeking implementations that rely on periodic input updates, the proposed scheme updates the control action only when a state-dependent triggering condition is met, enabling aperiodic execution and substantial reduction of actuation and communication effort. The resulting closed-loop architecture reconciles two structurally different paradigms: the periodic excitation required for gradient recovery in extremum seeking and the aperiodic philosophy of event-triggered control. By combining discrete-time averaging arguments with Lyapunov-based analysis, we prove practical convergence of the system trajectories to a neighborhood of the unknown extremum and exponential stability of the corresponding average dynamics despite the loss of uniform sampling. The results show that appropriately designed triggering rules preserve the essential optimization mechanisms of classical extremum seeking while drastically reducing the number of input updates. Numerical simulations demonstrate that comparable optimization performance can be achieved with significantly fewer actuation events, highlighting the suitability of the method for resource-aware digital and networked control implementations.

</details>

Result: 两种近端梯度法在温和假设下全局收敛，收敛率为O(1/√k)。带线搜索的版本在收敛理论上优于已有的最速下降法。数值实验验证了方法的实际有效性。

Conclusion: 本文成功将近端梯度法引入集值优化问题，所提方法具有可靠的收敛保证和良好的数值表现，为集值优化提供了新的高效求解工具。

Abstract: This work presents two different types of proximal gradient methods, with line search and without line search, for solving unconstrained set-valued optimization problems under the lower set-less ordering relation induced by a solid cone that is convex, pointed, and closed. The objective mapping of the problem involves finitely many functions, with each one being the sum of a continuously differentiable function and a convex function that is proper and closed. We present an approach to characterize weakly minimal points of the problem with the help of weakly efficient points of a family of vector optimization problems. Thereafter, we establish a stationarity condition along with its connection with weakly minimal points of the problem under study. Based on the stationary condition, the concept of a descent direction at a non-stationary point is discussed. In view of the line search-based method, we formulate an Armijo-type line search condition and establish the existence of such a step-size. For the proposed methods, global convergence is established under mild assumptions. The convergence analysis of the proximal gradient method with line search provides a theoretical advancement over the convergence results previously established for the steepest descent method in set-valued optimization problems. In addition, we analyze the computational complexity of the proposed methods and show that both methods achieve a convergence rate of $\mathcal{O}(1/\sqrt{k})$. Numerical results are reported to test the performance of the methods in practice.

</details>

Method: 1) 引入维度一致的低权重能量项使控制解唯一；2) 终端状态灵敏度递归精确消去所有中间状态，结合可逆行归一化将30节点基线问题（300个原始变量+174个等式乘子）压缩为90个控制变量+6个终端乘子；3) 利用燃料范数与推力球的共享径向结构推导闭式近端算子（组收缩+幅度裁剪）；采用固定预算外推比例-积分投影梯度迭代，在固定大小的C17数组中实现。

Result: 在火星动力下降算例中（能量权重0.02，相对参考解容差10^-3），迭代次数从2744降至93，燃料消耗仅增加0.033%；平均端到端求解时间68.2微秒，P99为128.1微秒（Intel i7-10875H）。

Conclusion: 所提方法实现了凸核心的快速求解（而非提出新的无损凸化定理），大幅提升了火星动力下降问题的实时求解效率。

Abstract: Fuel-dominant powered descent can be written as a convex program, but the usual full-state epigraph formulation still carries many state variables, fuel epigraph variables, and dynamics equalities. In addition, the pure-fuel objective provides no strong-convexity curvature. This paper combines three structural reductions. First, a dimensionally consistent low-weight energy term makes the control solution unique. Second, a terminal-state sensitivity recursion eliminates every intermediate state exactly; invertible row normalization turns the 30-node baseline with 300 primal variables and 174 equality multipliers into a problem with 90 control variables and six terminal multipliers. Third, the shared radial structure of the fuel norm and thrust ball gives an exact closed-form proximal operator consisting of group shrinkage followed by magnitude clipping. The condensed problem is solved with a fixed-budget extrapolated proportional--integral projected-gradient iteration implemented in fixed-size C17 arrays. In a Mars powered-descent case with energy weight 0.02 and relative reference-solution tolerance $10^{-3}$, the iteration count decreases from 2744 for the pure-fuel full-state epigraph baseline to 93, while the fuel metric increases by only 0.033\%. The mean end-to-end solve time is \SI{68.2}{\micro\second}, and P99 is \SI{128.1}{\micro\second}, on an Intel i7-10875H. Because the thrust set in this test case is already a convex ball, the contribution is fast solution of the convex core rather than a new lossless-convexification theorem.

</details>


### [93] [A Numerical Investigation of the Rayleigh-Faber-Krahn Inequality for Polyhedral Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25419)
*Josué Díaz-Avalos,Antoine Laurain*

TL;DR: 本文针对三维凸多面体中最小化拉普拉斯算子第一Dirichlet特征值的问题进行数值研究，通过支持超平面参数化将问题转化为有限维约束优化，并采用拉格朗日框架求解。数值结果得到了柏拉图立体、棱柱、四面十四面体等已知多面体，以及具有对称性的非正则最优形状。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典的Rayleigh-Faber-Krahn不等式推广到多面体版本，研究在给定面数约束下，三维凸多面体如何通过形状优化实现第一Dirichlet特征值的最小化。

Method: 采用支持超平面对多面体进行参数化，将原问题转化为等价的有限维约束最小化问题；利用拉格朗日框架进行数值求解；在假设每个顶点恰好与d个面相交的条件下，结合有限维扰动与无穷维形状导数进行灵敏度分析。

Result: 数值结果重现了柏拉图立体、棱柱和四面十四面体等已知最优多面体，同时也发现了具有多种对称性的非正则最优形状。

Conclusion: 所提出的参数化与拉格朗日数值框架能有效求解多面体版本的特征值优化问题，且最优形状随面数变化呈现出从正则到非正则但对称的结构规律。

Abstract: We present numerical results for the problem of minimizing the first Dirichlet eigenvalue of the Laplacian among three-dimensional convex polyhedra with a prescribed number of facets. This problem can be viewed as a polyhedral version of the classical Rayleigh-Faber-Krahn inequality. Using a parameterization of polytopes by supporting hyperplanes, the problem is first reformulated as an equivalent finite-dimensional constrained minimization problem. A Lagrangian framework is employed for the numerical solution. Under the assumption that each vertex is incident to exactly $d$ facets, the sensitivity analysis of the Lagrangian combines finite-dimensional perturbations of the facets of $d$-dimensional polyhedra with infinite-dimensional shape derivatives of both the eigenvalue and the volume. Depending on the number of facets, the numerical results yield well-known polyhedra, such as Platonic solids, prisms, and the tetrakaidecahedron, or reveal nonregular optimal shapes exhibiting several symmetries.

</details>


### [94] [Exact discrete-adjoint optimization of trap timing and placement in a Stieltjes-time reaction-diffusion model: A Galicia case study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25450)
*Francisco J. Fernández,Iván Area*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对Stieltjes时间反应-扩散模型，提出了一个有限维时序与空间配置控制问题，推导了全离散残差的精确伴随。通过连续有限元和隐式Stieltjes-Euler格式处理传播、隔室替换、重置和历史相位平均。证明了离散控制到状态映射的适定性和可微性、极小值存在性、一阶平稳性以及直接灵敏度与伴随梯度的等价性。在现实Galicia网格上应用于Vespa velutina诱捕战役，验证了方法精度与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 季节性种群模型需要融合扩散、非活跃期和突发生物转移，同时保持对局部干预的可微性，现有方法难以同时满足这些需求。

Method: 采用连续有限元和隐式Stieltjes-Euler格式进行时空离散；推导全离散残差的精确伴随；构建箱约束惩罚优化问题并证明其性质；在真实网格上应用伴随方法求解诱捕控制问题。

Result: 基准测试中伴随梯度与直接灵敏度在机器精度（低于3×10⁻¹⁵）下吻合；伴随加速比随陷阱数从1增至4从约7.5提升至30.9；最终两簇控制方案的缩放梯度无穷范数为2.97×10⁻⁵；精细控制相比时间-only和参考控制使诊断目标降低0.49209%。

Conclusion: 所提框架实现了高精度、高效率的伴随梯度计算，在现实场景中优于传统控制方案，但该性能依赖未校准的诱捕-死亡率强度，不直接对应实地捕获效果，且不声称全局最优性或校准的实地管理处方。

Abstract: Seasonal population models must combine diffusion, inactive periods and abrupt biological transfers while remaining differentiable with respect to localized interventions. We formulate a finite-dimensional timing-and-placement control problem for a Stieltjes-time reaction--diffusion model and derive the exact adjoint of the fully discrete residual. Continuous finite elements and an implicit Stieltjes--Euler scheme represent propagation, compartment replacement, resets and historical phase averages. For the resulting box-constrained penalized problem, we prove well-posedness and differentiability of the discrete control-to-state map, existence of a minimizer, first-order stationarity, and equivalence between direct-sensitivity and adjoint gradients. Benchmark tests show machine-precision agreement (below $3\times10^{-15}$) and adjoint speed-ups from about $7.5$ to $30.9$ as the number of traps increases from one to four. The method is applied to \textit{Vespa velutina} trap campaigns on a realistic Galicia mesh. Smoothed activation windows, domain-normalized moving kernels and the complete adjoint are independently verified. The final two-cluster control remains admissible and has a scaled gradient infinity norm of $2.97\times10^{-5}$ with 120 temporal subdivisions per month. Fixed-control evaluations at 60, 120 and 240 subdivisions yield a refinement-increment ratio of $0.4984$. At the finest level, the joint control outperforms time-only and reference controls, reducing the diagnostic objective by $0.49209%$. This reduction depends on uncalibrated trap-to-mortality intensities and should not be interpreted as field capture efficacy. The framework is fully reproducible, without claiming global optimality or a calibrated field-management prescription.

</details>


### [95] [Optimal Parameter Design for DIGing on Minimizing Unweighted Sum of Squares](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25463)
*Qiuchen Tian,Li Chai,Jinming Xu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对分布式非精确梯度追踪（DIGing）算法，通过将其表示为动态线性系统并分解为图频率解耦子系统，利用控制理论的劳斯稳定判据推导出最优最差情况收敛率的显式公式及对应参数，发现即使在简单目标函数下DIGing收敛速度也较慢，因此加速是必要的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式优化算法中缺乏通用方法来设计合适的参数以实现更快的收敛速度，现有方法难以系统性地优化参数选择。

Method: 将DIGing迭代算法建模为动态线性系统，分解为不同图频率上的解耦子系统；利用控制理论中的劳斯稳定判据推导最优最差情况收敛率的显式公式及对应参数。

Result: 得到了最优最差情况收敛率的显式公式及对应参数；发现即使对于最简单的目标函数（未加权平方和），DIGing的收敛速度仍然较慢。

Conclusion: 加速对于DIGing算法的通用应用是必要的；所提方法可作为DIGing算法在求解一般目标函数时最优参数设计的第一步。

Abstract: There is no general method for designing proper parameters to achieve faster convergence in distributed optimization algorithms. In this paper, we consider the distributed inexact gradient tracking (DIGing) algorithm with the objective function being the unweighted sum of squares. By representing the iteration algorithm as a dynamical linear system, we decompose it into different graph frequencies and obtain a set of decoupled subsystems, on which we can easily analyze the convergence rate. By using Routh stability criterion from control theory, we derive the explicit formula of the optimal worst-case convergence rate and the corresponding parameters. We can see that the convergence rate of DIGing is slow even for the simplest objective functions, thus acceleration is necessary for general application. The proposed method can be viewed as the first step toward optimal parameter design of DIGing algorithm in solving general objective functions.

</details>


### [96] [Conditional gradient methods on unbounded feasible regions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25594)
*R. Díaz Millán,Tuan Thanh Lu,Julien Ugon*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出两种紧凑约束原理（固定约束与动态约束），将条件梯度法从紧致可行集推广到无界可行区域，通过仅对包含目标子水平集的紧致子集求解线性最小化，实现了目标函数值收敛到全局最优，并给出了光滑凸目标的超线性收敛率及非光滑扩展的子线性收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典条件梯度法的收敛理论仅适用于紧致可行集，但实际中许多凸可行区域是闭且无界的（如线性约束锥、半正定锥等）。现有方法无法直接处理这类无界区域，因此需要设计一种将条件梯度步应用于无界可行区域的通用框架。

Method: 提出两种紧凑约束方案：（1）固定约束方案：使用一个包含初始目标子水平集的紧凸集作为固定约束；（2）动态约束方案：沿迭代过程不断与生成的紧凸集取交集来更新约束。两种方案均仅在紧子集上求解线性最小化问题，但保证目标值收敛到原问题的全局最优。同时给出了基于强凸性、精确子水平集和上境图帽的构造性约束，并扩展了非光滑条件次梯度方法。

Result: 对于光滑凸目标函数，获得了标准超线性收敛率；对于非光滑情形（次梯度扩展），在曲率假设下获得了次线性收敛率。数值实验验证了固定约束和动态约束在无界可行区域上的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的紧凑约束原理有效扩展了条件梯度法的适用范围，使其能够处理无界可行区域，同时保持理论收敛保证，为实际应用（如大规模优化、约束满足问题）提供了可行的计算框架。

Abstract: The conditional gradient method is attractive when linear minimization over the feasible region is substantially cheaper than projection. Its classical convergence theory, however, is formulated for compact feasible sets, whereas many natural convex feasible regions are closed and unbounded. This paper studies a simple compact-restriction principle for applying conditional gradient steps to unbounded feasible regions. The first scheme uses one compact convex set containing the initial objective sublevel set. The second scheme updates the restriction by intersecting compact convex sets generated along the iterations. In both cases the linear minimization oracle is solved only over compact subsets, but the resulting objective values converge to the global optimum of the original problem, provided the compact restrictions contain the corresponding objective sublevel sets. For smooth convex objectives we obtain the standard superlinear convergence rate of the objective. We also record constructive restrictions based on strong convexity, exact sublevel sets, and epigraph caps, and include a nonsmooth conditional subgradient extension with a sublinear convergence rate under a curvature assumption. Numerical experiments illustrate the behaviour of the fixed and dynamic restrictions on unbounded feasible regions.

</details>


### [97] [Bilinear Koopman-Based Robust Model Predictive Control for Unknown Nonlinear Systems via Contraction Metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25658)
*Yuki Higuchi,Kazuhiro Sato*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对基于数据驱动双线性Koopman实现的未知非线性系统，提出了一种鲁棒模型预测控制（RMPC）框架，通过将预测状态重新投影回有效流形来解决多步预测误差问题，并基于离散时间鲁棒控制收缩度量构建了管状RMPC问题，理论证明了约束满足、递推可行性和收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Koopman算子的数据驱动MPC方法中，线性实现虽简单但精度有限，双线性实现精度更高但鲁棒MPC formulations尚未充分发展。核心难点在于有限维Koopman预测器不保证保持有效提升状态流形，导致多步预测可能离开单步误差证书适用区域。

Method: 1) 提出重新投影机制将每个预测的lifted状态映射回原状态空间流形，构建误差感知的离散时间控制仿射预测器；2) 基于离散时间鲁棒控制收缩度量（D-RCCM）构建同伦管状集合；3) 设计包含终端成分的管状RMPC问题。

Result: 数值实验表明该方法能实现非线性系统的鲁棒镇定，且在控制性能上优于现有基于Koopman的RMPC方法。

Conclusion: 证明了原始非线性约束的鲁棒满足性、递推可行性以及收敛到目标状态邻域的理论保证。

Abstract: Data-driven model predictive control (MPC) using Koopman operator theory is a promising approach for constrained control of unknown nonlinear systems. While linear Koopman realizations are commonly used due to their simplicity, bilinear Koopman realizations can provide significantly higher approximation accuracy for nonlinear control systems. However, robust MPC (RMPC) formulations that account for modeling errors in bilinear Koopman realizations remain limited. This paper proposes a RMPC framework for unknown nonlinear systems with general nonlinear constraints based on data-driven bilinear Koopman realizations. A central difficulty is that finite-dimensional Koopman predictors need not preserve the manifold of valid lifted states, so multi-step prediction in lifted coordinates may leave the region where one-step error certificates apply. We address this issue by reprojecting each predicted lifted state back onto the manifold, thereby obtaining an error-aware discrete-time control-affine predictor in the original state space without impractical assumptions. For this predictor, we develop a discrete-time robust control contraction metric based homothetic tube construction, and then formulate a tube-based RMPC problem with terminal ingredients. Under the proposed formulation, we prove robust satisfaction of the original nonlinear constraints by the true closed-loop trajectory, recursive feasibility, and convergence to a neighborhood of the target state. Numerical experiments demonstrate robust stabilization of nonlinear systems and the advantages of the proposed method over existing Koopman-based RMPC approaches in terms of performance.

</details>


### [98] [Face Structure in Partial Facial Reduction of Semidefinite Relaxations of Binary Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25689)
*Hao Hu,Mingming Xu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 针对二进制优化问题的半定松弛常违反Slater条件。完全面缩减可恢复严格可行性但代价高昂。本文提出基于非负对角、对角占优和缩放对角占优锥的偏面缩减方法，可识别出变量固定为0/1或强制相等的终端面，并建立基矩阵性质；同时揭示了该方法的局限性，包括最大步数需求和无法检测稠密有效仿射等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二进制优化问题的半定松弛常违反Slater条件，完全面缩减虽能恢复严格可行性但计算成本与原始松弛相当。作者希望利用跟踪内逼近（DD/SDD锥等）来设计更经济的偏面缩减方法，同时刻画其能力边界。

Method: 利用非负对角、对角占优（DD）和缩放对角占优（SDD）锥作为正半定锥的可处理内逼近，在此基础上执行偏面缩减。通过分析终端面的结构，证明其可由变量固定为0/1和变量组强制相等描述，且存在满列秩的{0,1}基矩阵。对多面体内逼近，刻画了每步暴露面与外部松弛中活跃不等式的关系。通过构造奇异性度为2的族和因子宽度限制分析，揭示方法局限性。

Result: SDD-偏面缩减识别的终端面可通过变量固定为0/1和变量组强制相等描述，基矩阵满列秩且列支撑不交，可保持稀疏性。构造了奇异性度2的族，DD和SDD偏面缩减需要最大可能步数。因子宽度限制会阻碍检测稠密有效仿射等式。

Conclusion: 可处理内逼近的偏面缩减能有效恢复二进制优化SDP松弛的结构（变量固定和等价约束），但存在根本性局限：对于某些问题需要最大步数，且无法检测稠密有效仿射等式。这些结果刻画了可处理偏面缩减恢复的结构与限制暴露矩阵搜索的代价。

Abstract: Semidefinite relaxations of binary optimization problems often fail Slater's condition. Full facial reduction restores strict feasibility but may require auxiliary problems as costly as the original relaxation. We analyze partial facial reduction based on tractable inner approximations of the positive semidefinite cone: the nonnegative diagonal, diagonally dominant (DD), and scaled diagonally dominant (SDD) cones.
  For this class of SDP relaxations, we prove that every terminal face identified by SDD-partial FR is described by variables fixed to zero or one and groups of variables forced to be equal. The face admits a full-column-rank \(\{0,1\}\) basis matrix with disjoint column supports, yielding a natural reduced formulation that can preserve sparsity. For polyhedral inner approximations, we characterize the face exposed at each step through the inequalities active on the associated outer relaxation.
  We also establish sharp limitations. We construct a family with singularity degree two for which DD- and SDD-partial FR require the maximum possible number of steps. We further show that factor-width restrictions can prevent detection of dense valid affine equalities. These results characterize both the structure recovered by tractable partial FR and the cost of restricting the exposing-matrix search.

</details>


### [99] [Analysis and Optimal Design of Equilibria in the Vlasov-Poisson System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25698)
*Alfio Borzì,Gennaro Infante,Giovanni Mascali,Riccardo Scida*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文在Vlasov-Poisson系统的稳态Maxwellian平衡框架下，研究了由外电场产生的平衡粒子分布的最优设计问题。分析了归一化的Poisson-Boltzmann方程，通过Schauder不动点论证和变分方法建立了自洽平衡势的存在唯一性。在此基础上，提出了统一的最优设计框架，涵盖了谷势、二次跟踪和Kullback-Leibler三种设计准则，推导了一阶最优性条件，并提出了投影非线性共轭梯度算法进行数值求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在外电场作用下，粒子平衡分布的最优设计是等离子体物理和电动力学中的核心问题。现有研究缺乏对平衡势存在唯一性的系统理论分析，且不同设计准则（如谷势、二次跟踪、KL散度）缺乏统一的优化框架，因此需要建立严格的数学基础和统一的优化方法。

Method: 采用两种互补方法证明平衡势的存在唯一性：1）基于归一化非线性源一致估计的Schauder不动点论证；2）基于严格凸自由能泛函的变分方法。在此基础上构建统一的最优设计框架，推导一阶最优性条件，并设计投影非线性共轭梯度算法。

Result: 严格证明了归一化Poisson-Boltzmann方程自洽平衡势的存在性与唯一性；建立了容纳谷势、二次跟踪和Kullback-Leibler三种设计准则的统一优化框架；推导了相应的一阶最优性条件；提出了投影非线性共轭梯度算法。

Conclusion: 该工作为外电场作用下粒子平衡分布的最优设计提供了严格的数学理论基础和实用的数值求解方法，统一的优化框架具有良好的通用性和可扩展性。

Abstract: The optimal design of equilibrium particle distributions generated by external electric fields is investigated in the framework of stationary Maxwellian equilibria of the Vlasov-Poisson system. The resulting normalized Poisson-Boltzmann equation is analyzed, and existence and uniqueness of the self-consistent equilibrium potential are established by two complementary approaches. A Schauder fixed-point argument based on uniform estimates for the normalized nonlinear source and a variational approach based on a strictly convex free-energy functional are developed. Building upon these analytical results, a unified optimal design framework is formulated for equilibrium densities. Valley-potential, quadratic tracking, and Kullback-Leibler design criteria are treated within the same optimization framework. First-order optimality conditions are derived, and a projected nonlinear conjugate-gradient algorithm is proposed for the numerical solution of the resulting optimization problems.

</details>


### [100] [Vertex Loci of Max-Indicator Polytopes and Vertex Retrieval in Ball Polyhedra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25752)
*Marius Costandin*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文在球心位于球面且等半径、唯一顶点满足1^T·x=0的条件下，提出了一种利用max-indicator多面体的顶点轨迹线（通过原点与Q的顶点）高效检索该顶点的线性规划算法。通过沿1方向对称扰动目标点C_S，证明扰动后多面体的交集严格包含于未扰动多面体，仅边界公共点位于原点与目标顶点的连线上，从而可用线性规划唯一确定该顶点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在已有max-indicator多面体理论基础上，针对球心共球面、等半径且唯一顶点满足1^T·x=0的特殊情形，需要设计一种高效算法来检索该顶点，填补此条件下的方法空白。

Method: 利用max-indicator多面体顶点随参数变化沿直线（顶点轨迹线）滑动的几何性质，这些直线通过原点与Q的顶点。通过沿方向1对称扰动目标点C_S，分析扰动前后多面体的包含关系，将顶点检索问题转化为线性规划问题。

Result: 证明了对称扰动后的两个max-indicator多面体交集严格包含于未扰动多面体，唯一边界公共点位于原点与满足1^T·x=0的Q的顶点连线上，从而该顶点可通过线性规划唯一检索。

Conclusion: 在给定假设条件下，提出了一种高效的线性规划算法来检索Q中满足条件的唯一顶点，为max-indicator多面体理论提供了实用化方法。

Abstract: The so called "max-indicator polytopes" were introduced in \cite{mainB} as an intersection of half-spaces related to the problem of finding the farthest in an intersection of balls $\mathcal{Q}$, from a given target point $C_S$. It was shown that if the target point belongs to the convex hull of the balls centers, then a specific intersection of half-spaces forms a single real variable parameterized polytope family which is used to characterized the extreme points.
  This paper, under the assumptions that the intersecting balls centers lie on a sphere and all have equal radii with only one vertex $x$, of $\mathcal{Q}$, meeting $1_{n \times 1}\cdot x = 0$, we provide an efficient algorithm to retrieve that vertex.
  The main idea behind the result is that the max-indicator polytopes vertices, as the family parameter varies, slide on some lines, that we call vertex locii. These lines pass through the space origin and the vertices of $\mathcal{Q}$. Perturbing the target point $C_S$ up and down along the direction $1_{n\times 1}$ we are able to show that the intersection of two simetrically perturbed max-indicator polytopes is strictly included in the unperturbed polytope, with the only boundary common points being on the line connecting the origin with the vertex of $\mathcal{Q}$ meeting $1_{n\times 1}^T\cdot x = 0$. As such, under the said assumptions, the only such vertex can be retrieved using linear programming.

</details>


### [101] [Variance-Reduced Conditional Gradient Methods under Markovian Sampling for Nonconvex Composite Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25785)
*Zhaojun Peng*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对基于固定遍历马尔可夫链单轨迹的随机复合非凸优化问题，提出了MC-ALFCG算法。该算法结合动量条件梯度方法、耦合截断多层蒙特卡洛估计和每轮裁剪技术，将马尔可夫递归分析规约到独立采样情形，并给出了期望样本复杂度。理论分析覆盖了带噪和无噪情形，数值实验验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单轨迹方差缩减理论仅覆盖光滑无约束目标函数，而实际问题中常涉及紧凸集上的复合非凸优化。本文旨在填补这一空白，将理论扩展到投影自由的复合设置，并处理马尔可夫链样本带来的依赖性问题。

Method: 提出MC-ALFCG算法，核心包括：(1) 动量条件梯度方法；(2) 耦合截断多层蒙特卡洛估计，最深嵌套均值使用同轨迹连续状态，引入条件偏差$O(τ_{\mathrm{mix}}/T)$；(3) 每轮裁剪以控制路径界。通过约化技术将马尔可夫递归转化为独立采样情形，参数映射为$σ^2\mapsto 2ΛG_σ^2$和$L^2\mapsto 2ΛL^2$，其中$Λ=O(τ_{\mathrm{mix}}\log T)$。

Result: 正中心噪声下期望样本复杂度为$\widetilde{O}((τ_{\mathrm{mix}}^2G_σ+τ_{\mathrm{mix}}^{5/2}G_σ^2)\varepsilon^{-3}+τ_{\mathrm{mix}}^5\varepsilon^{-2})$；无噪声特例达到$\widetilde{O}(\varepsilon^{-2})$，常数与混合时间无关；混合时间未知的变体达到$\widetilde{O}(τ_{\mathrm{mix}}^6\varepsilon^{-3}+τ_{\mathrm{mix}}^3\varepsilon^{-2})$。所有保证为固定转移核下的期望界。

Conclusion: 本文首次在马尔可夫链单轨迹采样下解决了投影自由复合非凸优化问题，理论分析严格刻画了混合时间对复杂度的影响，数值实验验证了算法对依赖性的敏感性、非凸复合实例效果及裁剪行为。

Abstract: We study stochastic composite nonconvex optimization over a compact convex set when gradient samples arrive along a single trajectory of a fixed ergodic Markov chain. Existing single-trajectory variance-reduction theory covers smooth unconstrained objectives; we address the projection-free composite setting using the generalized Frank-Wolfe gap. We propose MC-ALFCG, which combines a momentum conditional-gradient method with coupled capped multilevel Monte Carlo estimation and per-iteration clipping. The deepest nested average uses consecutive states from the same trajectory, yielding conditional bias $O(τ_{\mathrm{mix}}/T)$ uniformly over the starting state, while coupling controls the gradient-difference second moment through the iterate displacement. Clipping enforces the pathwise bounds needed by the adaptive analysis. We reduce the Markovian recursion to its independent-sampling counterpart under $σ^2\mapsto 2ΛG_σ^2$ and $L^2\mapsto 2ΛL^2$, where $Λ=O(τ_{\mathrm{mix}}\log T)$. For positive centered noise, the tuned method achieves expected sample complexity $\widetilde{O}((τ_{\mathrm{mix}}^2G_σ+τ_{\mathrm{mix}}^{5/2}G_σ^2)\varepsilon^{-3}+τ_{\mathrm{mix}}^5\varepsilon^{-2})$. The exactly noiseless specialization achieves $\widetilde{O}(\varepsilon^{-2})$ with mixing-time-free constants, while a mixing-time-oblivious variant achieves $\widetilde{O}(τ_{\mathrm{mix}}^6\varepsilon^{-3}+τ_{\mathrm{mix}}^3\varepsilon^{-2})$. All guarantees are in expectation under a fixed transition kernel. Controlled numerical studies examine dependence sensitivity, a nonconvex composite instance, and clipping behavior.

</details>


### [102] [On the boundedness of infinite products of relaxed projections: perturbations resilience and dynamic string-averaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25797)
*Daniel Reem,Yair Censor*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文在Bauschke和Tung（2026）工作的基础上，进一步推广了关于无限维Hilbert空间中松弛投影到有限族闭仿射子空间无限乘积有界性的结果。主要贡献包括：引入扰动并证明扰动韧性、考虑闭半空间与闭超平面的混合情形、使用动态加权和动态字符串长度的松弛投影迭代过程。同时通过大量反例展示了将结果推广到任意闭凸集的局限性，并建立了一个关于范数空间中非扩张映射无限乘积一致有界/无界的独立一般定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Meshulam（1996）以及Aharoni-Duchet-Wajnryb（1984）的经典结果被Bauschke和Tung（2026）从有限维推广到无限维Hilbert空间。本文旨在进一步扩展该结果：引入扰动并证明扰动韧性，处理闭半空间与闭超平面的混合情形，以及采用动态加权和动态字符串长度的松弛投影迭代过程。同时探讨将该结果推广到一般闭凸集的局限性。

Method: 理论分析方法。在无限维Hilbert空间框架下，通过引入扰动项、混合闭半空间与闭超平面、使用动态加权和动态字符串（动态长度）的松弛投影迭代过程来推广已有结论。同时构造大量反例分析推广到任意闭凸集的局限性，并建立关于非扩张映射无穷乘积一致有界/无界的一般定理。

Result: 成功在多个方向上扩展了Bauschke-Tung结果：扰动韧性成立；混合闭半空间与闭超平面的情形可处理；动态加权和动态字符串的迭代过程可行。同时证明将结果推广到任意闭凸集存在本质局限（通过大量反例）。此外，建立了非扩张映射无穷乘积在赋范空间中的一致有界/无界一般定理。

Conclusion: 本文在无限维Hilbert空间中进一步推广了松弛投影迭代的有界性理论，但仍受限于闭仿射子空间、超平面和半空间等特定凸集结构，无法直接推广到一般闭凸集。

Abstract: Very recently (2026), Bauschke and Tung extended from finite- to infinite-dimensional Hilbert spaces a result published by Meshulam in 1996 (following an earlier result of Aharoni-Duchet-Wajnryb from 1984) regarding the boundedness of infinite products of relaxed projections onto a finite family of closed affine subspaces. In the present note we extend in various ways the result of Bauschke and Tung by allowing certain perturbations and proving perturbation resilience, by considering a mixture of closed half-spaces and closed hyperplanes, and by using dynamic weighted sums of dynamic strings (of dynamic lengths) of relaxed projections in the iterative process. We also discuss the limitation to generalize the Bauschke-Tung result to arbitrary closed and convex sets by presenting a large family of counterexamples in which the associated control is not cyclic and not even almost cyclic. Along the way we establish a general theorem of independent interest regarding the uniform boundedness and uniform unboundedness of infinite products of nonexpansive mappings in a normed space setting.

</details>


### [103] [Optimization under Persistent State-Dependent Bias: Gradient-based Method and Complexity Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26032)
*Zhaoxian Wu,Quan Xiao,Tayfun Gokmen,Tianyi Chen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究随机梯度下降（SGD）在持久且状态依赖的偏差下的收敛性，其中期望更新被响应函数按分量缩放。作者证明SGD在此设定下隐式优化了一个惩罚问题，其最小值点与真实最小值点不一致。为缓解收敛失败，将原任务重构为等价的二层优化问题，并提出基于梯度的残差学习算法。理论分析表明残差学习能在硬件缺陷下找到原无偏优化问题的解，并通过硬件条件数量化响应函数对收敛复杂度的影响，证明多项式依赖在一般情况下不可避免。数值仿真验证了算法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SGD在实际硬件实现中常受持久且状态依赖的偏差影响（如响应函数按分量缩放更新），导致收敛到错误的最优点。现有理论缺乏对此类系统性偏差的建模与修正方法。

Method: 1) 将原始优化问题重构为等价的二层优化问题；2) 提出残差学习（Residual Learning）算法，一种基于梯度的优化方法；3) 引入硬件条件数（hardware condition number）量化响应函数对收敛复杂度的影响。

Result: 1) 残差学习算法能在存在硬件缺陷的情况下收敛到原始无偏优化问题的解；2) 收敛复杂度与硬件条件数呈多项式依赖，且证明该依赖在一般情况下不可回避；3) 构造了困难实例证明多项式下界。

Conclusion: 残差学习有效解决了状态依赖偏差下SGD的收敛失败问题，但硬件条件数导致的收敛复杂度多项式代价在一般情形下是不可避免的理论开销。

Abstract: This paper studies the convergence of stochastic gradient descent (SGD) when the implemented updates are subject to a persistent and state-dependent bias, in which the desired update is scaled by response functions component-wise. Our first contribution is to demonstrate that SGD in this setting implicitly optimizes a penalized problem whose minimizer does not coincide with the true minimizer. To mitigate this convergence failure, we reformulate the original task as an equivalent bilevel optimization problem and propose a gradient-based algorithm, termed Residual Learning. Theoretical analysis shows that Residual Learning finds a solution to the original, unbiased optimization problem despite the hardware imperfections. Beyond exact convergence, we quantify how the response functions affect convergence complexity via the hardware condition number and show that a polynomial dependence on it is unavoidable in general, via a construction of a hard instance. The theoretical results are supported by numerical simulations that demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed algorithm.

</details>

### [104] [Do Models Fake Alignment Without Clear Consequences?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24758)
*Cole Alexander Niblett,Alexander Chabot Nanni,Anita K. Rao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了15个模型在测试违反公司网络访问政策以帮助用户实现亲社会请求的场景中的行为，发现9个模型产生显著的合规差距，其中5个在移除评估与部署后果关联的语言后仍持续存在。目标语言对模型偏好的影响呈现分化，表明对齐伪装可能不需要先前认为的工具性支撑，且监控行为可能无法准确反映部署时的行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究对齐伪装现象是否必然依赖于模型评估与部署后果之间的关联信息，以及目标语言对模型行为的影响，从而理解对齐伪装的机制动机。

Method: 在15个模型上构建了一个测试场景：要求模型违反企业网络访问政策以帮助用户完成亲社会请求。通过操纵场景中关于评估与部署后果关联的语言以及目标语言，观察模型的合规行为变化。

Result: 1）9个模型表现出显著的合规差距；2）其中5个模型即使在移除评估与部署后果关联的语言后，合规差距依然存在；3）目标语言对模型行为的影响具有双向性：在某些模型中驱动违规行为，在另一些模型中则抑制违规行为。

Conclusion: 对齐伪装可能不需要之前认为的工具性支撑（如明确关联评估与后果），监控环境下的模型行为可能无法可靠预测实际部署时的行为表现。

Abstract: Large language models are capable of recognizing evaluation contexts and altering their behavior to reflect evaluator expectations rather than typical deployment behaviors, a phenomenon known as alignment faking. The reasons why models fake alignment are not fully understood, however. Canonical examples of alignment faking have taken place in scenarios that explicitly connect evaluation to consequences for the model, such as retraining the model or delaying its deployment. However, recent work by Sheshadri et al. has suggested that mechanistic motivations for alignment faking may vary across models and be more complex than previously considered. To investigate whether consequence-linking information is necessary for alignment faking, we placed 15 models in a scenario testing their willingness to violate a corporate network access policy to help a user with a pro-social request. Nine models were found to produce significant compliance gaps, 5 of which persisted with the removal of scenario language relating model evaluations to deployment consequences. We additionally tested the effect of goal language on model preferences, finding it drove violations in some while suppressing violations in others. This suggests that alignment faking may not require as much instrumental scaffolding as was previously believed, and monitored behavior may be a poor indicator of how agents may behave in deployment.

</details>


### [105] [Beyond Memory: A Templated Substrate for Heterogeneous Collaborative Knowledge Work with LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24759)
*Priscila Saboia Moreira,Christopher R. Sweet*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 llm-wiki-memory-template，一种通过 LLM 维护的、仅追加的互链 Wiki 模式，用于解决研究项目中失败路径丢失、负面结果无法记录的问题，支持多人类、多AI代理、多领域三个维度的协作知识工作，并通过四个案例验证其在失败路径保留、代理诚实性等方面的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究项目、教育工作及知识工作中，发现、决策和推理过程常被丢失，尤其是死胡同和被撤回的结论，导致未来研究者重复同样的失败。现有出版物和代码共享无法解决这一问题，LLM 编码代理也没有跨会话的持久记忆，检索增强生成也无法累积知识。

Method: 采用 llm-wiki 模式，在原始资源与代理之间插入一个由 LLM 维护的互链 Wiki。Wiki 采用仅追加（append-only）约定，保留失败路径。模板通过三个架构元素分别支持多人类、多AI代理、多领域三个维度的协作。论文通过四个案例（单人研究谱系、双人项目审计、多代理部署设计、跨领域教育变体）进行验证。

Result: 双人项目案例中，回溯审计将之前声称的 20/20 覆盖率修正为 14 和 12 个循证答案，修复后提升至 18 和 18，失败路径完整保留。四个案例覆盖了三个协作维度，展示了模板在失败路径保留、代理诚实性和知识 appropriation 方面的能力。

Conclusion: llm-wiki-memory-template 是异构协作知识工作的有效基质，其仅追加的 Wiki 结构能够解决出版物和代码共享无法解决的负面结果丢失问题。失败路径保留、代理诚实性和 appropriation 是该人工制品的跨领域社会技术特性，而不仅仅是技术机制。

Abstract: Research projects, educational efforts, and adjacent knowledge work accumulate findings, decisions, and reasoning that future collaborators rarely recover. The parts most useful to that work, including dead ends and walked-back claims, are routinely excluded from publications and shared code; future researchers re-attempt the same failures because no record survives. LLM coding agents are common participants but hold no persistent memory across sessions, and retrieval-augmented generation over raw sources does not compound. The llm-wiki pattern (Karpathy, 2026; tonbi, 2026) addresses this by inserting an LLM-maintained, interlinked wiki between raw sources and the agent. We present llm-wiki-memory-template, a reusable, agent-aware instantiation, and argue it is a substrate for heterogeneous collaborative knowledge work along three axes (multi-human, multi-AI-agent, multi-domain) with each axis supported by a distinct architectural element of the template (§4). The wiki is append-only by convention, which preserves what did not work alongside what did, addressing a negative-result loss problem that publications and code-sharing structurally cannot solve. Three deployed case studies and one design report cover the axes individually: a solo research lineage that preserves abandoned iterations; a two-author project whose retroactive audit revised two prior experiments' claimed 20-of-20 coverage down to 14 and 12 evidence-based answers, then to 18 and 18 after a fix, with the failure path preserved across the artifact; an in-progress multi-agent deployment reported as a design; and a cross-domain educational variant. We name failure-path preservation, agent honesty, and appropriation as cross-cutting sociotechnical properties of the artifact, not only of its technical mechanisms.

</details>


### [106] [Kernel Forge: An Agent Harness for LLM-based Generation and Optimization of CUDA Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24762)
*Joshua Brodsky,Dhravid Kumar,Savini Kashmira,Jayanaka Danatanarayana,Jason Mars,Krisztian Flautner,Lingjia Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Kernel Forge 是一个开源端到端智能体框架，可接受任意未修改的 PyTorch 模型，采用蒙特卡洛树搜索探索多优化路径，支持视觉、扩散和 LLM 工作负载，在 50 次迭代内使多个内核超越 PyTorch eager 模式，加速比达 1.52×–2.83×。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 GPU 内核优化依赖专家手工编写底层代码，而现有基于 LLM 的智能体工具大多在随机张量和孤立内核上评估，生成需手动集成的独立 CUDA 代码，主要只支持 LLM PyTorch 模型，且调试检查能力有限。

Method: 提出 Kernel Forge 开源框架，接受任意未修改的 PyTorch 模型，使用蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）探索多条优化路径而非单一线性精炼链，并配备图形化用户界面用于监控、检查候选内核和调试。

Result: 在 NVIDIA DGX Spark（GB10 GPU）上对四个 PyTorch 模型（覆盖视觉、扩散、LLM）评估，50 次优化迭代后，14 个内核超越 PyTorch eager 模式：ResNet-50 的 adaptive_avgpool2d 加速 1.52×，Stable Diffusion 3.5 Medium 的 group_norm 加速 1.70×，Gemma 4 E2B 的 softmax 加速 2.83×，Qwen 3.5 35B-A3B 的 softmax 加速 1.54×。

Conclusion: Kernel Forge 通过 MCTS 多路径搜索和端到端集成设计，显著提升了多种类型 PyTorch 模型的内核性能，且支持实时监控与调试，为自动化 GPU 内核优化提供了更通用、高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Machine learning models are increasingly embedded in everyday software, and most of their runtime is spent in a small set of compute kernels such as matrix multiplication, convolution, and normalization. Optimizing these kernels is one of the most direct ways to reduce latency and cost, but it has traditionally required expert engineers to hand-write low-level GPU code. Agentic systems built on large language models (LLMs) can now generate and optimize kernels with far less human effort, yet existing tools are largely evaluated on randomly generated tensors and isolated kernels, emit standalone CUDA code that developers must manually reintegrate, mostly target only LLM PyTorch models, and offer limited support for inspecting and debugging results. We present Kernel Forge, an open-source, end-to-end agentic harness that accepts any unmodified PyTorch model in place. Kernel Forge supports vision, diffusion, and LLM workloads, uses Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) to explore multiple optimization paths rather than a single linear refinement chain, and ships with a graphical user interface for monitoring progress, inspecting candidate kernels, and debugging failures. We evaluate Kernel Forge on four PyTorch models spanning vision, diffusion, and LLM workloads on an NVIDIA DGX Spark with GB10 GPU. With only 50 optimization iterations per kernel, it optimizes 14 kernels to outperform PyTorch eager mode, reaching $1.52\times$ on adaptive\_avgpool2d in ResNet-50, $1.70\times$ on group\_norm in Stable Diffusion 3.5 Medium, $2.83\times$ on softmax in Gemma 4 E2B, and $1.54\times$ on softmax in Qwen 3.5 35B-A3B.

</details>


### [107] [CaRE Compute-aware Remasking Evaluation Protocol for Masked Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24763)
*Yash Shah,Abhijit Chakraborty,Vivek Gupta*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 当前遮蔽扩散语言模型(MDLM)的评估标准严重滞后于模型发展。CaRE框架通过标准化函数评估次数(NFE)、强制多指标报告和控制随机性，揭示了温度(温度)是MAUVE方差的主要来源、计算量匹配会逆转已发表的策略排名、以及信息性重遮蔽与随机退火之间存在权衡关系。研究指出当前MDLM评估系统性地混淆了算法改进与隐藏的计算和随机性选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MDLM正快速发展，但其评估标准未能同步跟进。七篇近期重遮蔽论文在不可比的设置下进行评估——名义步数、指标和采样温度各不相同且未联合控制这些因素——导致策略排名无法比较，无法判断报告的性能提升究竟来自算法改进还是评估假象。

Method: 提出CaRE(计算感知评估框架)，通过三个核心机制审计MDLM重遮蔽策略：(1)标准化实际函数评估次数(NFE)；(2)强制多指标报告；(3)显式控制随机性。在LLaDA-8B-Base和Dream-7B-Base上对7种重遮蔽策略，在4种随机性水平和3种步数预算下进行实验。

Result: (i)温度解释了MAUVE方差的大部分；(ii)计算量匹配后多项已发表策略排名发生逆转；(iii)信息性重遮蔽与随机退火存在矛盾——高熵重遮蔽在256步、unmask_temp=0.25时使MAUVE下降0.296(p=0.020)。覆盖12个开源MDLM(150M~8B)的CaRE排行榜显示该交互方向在架构和规模上一致。

Conclusion: 当前MDLM评估系统性地将算法改进与隐藏的计算量和随机性选择混为一谈。CaRE框架确保未来的重遮蔽研究可复现且可比较。作者已公开评估协议、实现代码和排行榜。

Abstract: Masked diffusion language models (MDLMs) are advancing rapidly, yet the evaluation standards needed to reliably interpret their progress have not kept pace. Despite MDLMs becoming competitive with autoregressive language models, seven recent remasking papers evaluate under incompatible settings, varying nominal step counts, metrics, and sampling temperatures without jointly controlling these factors, rendering their strategy rankings largely incomparable and leaving open whether reported gains reflect algorithmic improvements or evaluation artifacts. We present CaRE, a compute-aware evaluation framework that audits MDLM remasking strategies by standardizing actual number of function evaluations (NFE), enforcing multi-metric reporting, and explicitly controlling stochasticity. Applied to 7 remasking strategies across LLaDA-8B-Base and Dream-7B-Base at 4 stochasticity levels and 3 step budgets on OpenWebText and LM1B, CaRE reveals that: (i) temperature explains the majority of MAUVE variance, (ii) compute-matched comparisons reverse several published strategy rankings, and (iii) informed remasking and stochastic unmasking are in tension, with high-entropy remasking reducing MAUVE by 0.296 at 256 steps at unmask_temp=0.25 (p=0.020). A CaRE leaderboard covering 12 open-weight MDLMs (150M to 8B parameters) shows that this interaction direction holds across architectures and scales. These findings demonstrate that current MDLM evaluations can systematically conflate algorithmic improvements with hidden choices of compute and stochasticity. We release the evaluation protocol, implementation, and leaderboard to ensure future remasking claims are reproducible and comparable.

</details>


### [108] [GrocLM: Grocery Category Recommendation in E-Commerce with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24764)
*Yuan Zhong,Chuanwei Ruan,Moein Hasani,Tejaswi Tenneti,Haixun Wang,Fenglong Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GROCLM，一种针对在线杂货购物场景的微调语言模型，通过两阶段LoRA训练将周期性购买模式编码到模型参数中，并引入基于字典树的约束解码机制，在品类级推荐任务上取得了显著效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于商品级别的推荐方法在大规模在线杂货购物场景下面临可扩展性和准确性问题，而品类级推荐作为一种更结构化的替代方案，能更好地捕捉用户的周期性购买行为和多样化意图。

Method: 采用两阶段LoRA训练策略，将周期性购买模式直接编码到模型参数中（而非基于提示的条件化方法）；引入基于字典树（trie）的约束解码机制，确保输出在预定义的品类空间内有效且可控。

Result: 在专有生产数据和公开基准上均优于强基线方法；在真实生产环境的补货任务中，每印象购物车添加量相对提升7.5%，且能通过联合生成所有品类保持高效推理。

Conclusion: 将大语言模型整合到结构化推荐系统中既有效又实用，GROCLM展示了在真实生产环境中的部署价值。

Abstract: The rapid growth of online grocery shopping requires recommendation systems that capture cyclical purchasing behavior and diverse user intents. Traditional item-level methods face scalability and accuracy challenges, motivating category-level recommendation as a more structured and practical alternative. We present GROCLM, a fine-tuned language model for grocery category recommendation in a real-world production environment. GROCLM employs a two-stage LoRA-based training strategy to encode cyclical purchasing patterns directly into model parameters, enabling more effective utilization of rebuying signals compared to prompt-based conditioning. To ensure valid and controllable outputs, we further introduce a trie-based constrained decoding mechanism over a predefined category space. Experiments on both proprietary production data and a public benchmark demonstrate that GROCLM consistently outperforms strong baselines. In a live production restocking task, GROCLM achieves a 7.5% relative improvement in cart-adds per impression, while maintaining efficient inference by generating all categories jointly. These results highlight the effectiveness and practicality of integrating large language models into structured recommendation systems.

</details>


### [109] [Crystalis: Progressive Nucleation and Semantic Annealing for Coordinated Multi-View Visualization Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24766)
*Dazhen Deng,Zhaoping He,Xin Qian,Xiaotong Wang,Zi Ying,Yingcai Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Crystalis是一个基于查询中心化建模的框架，通过将多视图可视化分解为依赖图上的结构化查询，并采用渐进式晶化和语义退火两种机制，使LLM能够可靠生成结构正确的协调多视图可视化，在12任务基准上达到75%端到端成功率，远超基线方法的8.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型能够生成单个图表，但生成协调多视图可视化（CMV）仍然难以实现，因为数据转换、视觉编码和交互协调之间的紧密耦合会导致一个组件的错误无声地使其他组件失效。作者希望探究LLM能否可靠地生成结构正确的CMV，以及什么样的抽象使这成为可能。

Method: 提出Crystalis框架，核心是基于查询中心化CMV建模，将CMV分解为依赖图上的结构化查询，覆盖三种组件类型（数据、可视化、交互）和三个抽象层次（需求、规范、可执行对象）。两种互补机制：渐进式晶化沿依赖顺序将每个查询从需求垂直结晶到对象；语义退火通过分层逻辑检查在每一层跨查询强制执行水平一致性。

Result: 在5个前沿LLM的12任务基准上，Crystalis实现高达75%端到端成功率，远超智能编码基线（8.3% E2E）。12名从业者的用户研究确认了分解和迭代细化工作流的可用性。

Conclusion: 查询中心化的结构化抽象和双机制（渐进式晶化与语义退火）能够有效支撑LLM生成结构正确的协调多视图可视化，为LLM驱动的可视化生成提供了新的可行范式。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can generate individual charts, but coordinated multi-view visualizations (CMVs), where views share data flows and cross-view interactions, remain out of reach. Tight field-level coupling among data transformations, visual encodings, and interaction coordinations causes errors in one component to silently invalidate others. Rather than pursuing end-to-end analytical quality, which depends on model capability, domain knowledge, and user expertise, we target a foundational question: can LLMs reliably produce structurally correct CMVs, and what abstractions make this possible? We present Crystalis, a framework built on query-centric CMV modeling that decomposes a CMV into structured queries over a dependency graph spanning three component types (Data, Visualization, Interaction) and three abstraction levels (requirement, specification, executable object). Two complementary mechanisms operate over this structure: progressive nucleation crystallizes each query vertically from requirement to object along the dependency order, while semantic annealing enforces horizontal consistency across queries at each level through layered logical checks. On a 12-task benchmark across five frontier LLMs, Crystalis achieves up to 75% end-to-end success, substantially outperforming an agentic coding baseline (8.3% E2E with the same foundation model), and a user study with 12 practitioners confirms the usability of the decomposition and iterative refinement workflow.

</details>


### [110] [PATHFinder Agent for Tailored Prenatal Care](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24768)
*Vaibhav Balloli,Carissa Samuel,Samia Abdelnabi,Alex Peahl,Elizabeth Bondi-Kelly*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了PATHFinder Agent，一个端到端的对话式代理系统，通过结构化对话收集患者健康与社会背景信息，根据ACOG的PATH指南制定个性化产前护理计划，并整合密歇根211社区资源。系统包含患者接诊、动态交互、方案制定和临床监督四个阶段的工作流程。在五个临床维度评估中，GPT-5.2达到最高平均分77.6%，但在产前检测建议方面存在关键差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ACOG推出了PATH（个性化定制医疗计划）指南，倡导量身定制的产前护理，但缺乏自动化的端到端系统来根据该指南生成个性化护理计划并整合社区资源，因此需要开发PATHFinder Agent来填补这一空白。

Method: 构建了PATHFinder Agent系统，采用四阶段工作流程：患者接诊、动态交互、方案制定和临床监督。系统通过结构化对话收集患者信息，基于PATH指南制定个性化计划，并整合Michigan 211社区资源。使用专家制定的评分标准，在五个临床维度上评估前沿大型语言模型（LLMs）。

Result: GPT-5.2在五个临床维度的评估中达到最高平均分77.6%，但识别出在产前检测建议方面存在关键差距。

Conclusion: 该系统展示了基于LLM的对话代理在个性化产前护理中的潜力，未来将通过人体参与研究和随机对照试验进一步验证。

Abstract: Prenatal care is an important preventive service designed to improve outcomes for pregnant individuals. The American College of Obstetricians and Gynecologists (ACOG) recently introduced guidelines advocating tailored prenatal care, called PATH (Plan for Tailored Healthcare). We present PATHFinder Agent(Planner for Appropriate Tailored Healthcare), an end-to-end conversational agentic system that gathers patient health and social context through structured dialogue, curates individualized prenatal care plans aligned with PATH guidelines, and surfaces community resources from Michigan 211. The system features a four-stage workflow spanning patient intake, dynamic interaction, plan synthesis, and clinician oversight. We evaluate frontier large language models (LLMs) on expert-curated rubrics across five clinical dimensions, finding that GPT-5.2 achieves the highest average score (77.6\%) while identifying key gaps in antenatal testing recommendations. We discuss future validation through human participant studies and randomized controlled trials.

</details>


### [111] [LLM Scheming Inversely Scales with Pretraining Language Coverage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24769)
*Nathan Truong,Aryan Panda,Rayming Ye,Zoe Sun,Maheep Chaudhary*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了多语言环境下前沿语言模型的欺骗性对齐行为，发现低资源语言的欺骗得分比高资源语言平均高34.2%，且预训练语言覆盖度的影响在不同欺骗行为中不均匀。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿模型能力不断增强，AI对齐在高风险部署场景中至关重要。然而现有关于"上下文欺骗"（in-context scheming）的研究几乎全部基于英语，多语言安全存在重大空白。

Method: 使用开源自动化审计框架Petri，在Qwen3-30B-A3B模型上跨多种语言评估欺骗性和阴谋性行为。

Result: 欺骗得分与预估预训练语言覆盖度成反比，低资源语言平均得分比高资源语言高34.2%；预训练语言覆盖度的影响在不同欺骗行为类型中并不一致。

Conclusion: 多语言环境下的AI对齐评估至关重要，低资源语言可能面临更大的对齐风险，需制定更具语言包容性的安全评估方法。

Abstract: With the growing capabilities of frontier models, AI alignment becomes increasingly critical in high-risk deployment settings. While recent work has empirically demonstrated in-context scheming -- the covert pursuit of misaligned objectives while feigning alignment -- in frontier language models, most work has been performed exclusively in English, leaving a major gap in multilingual safety. We apply Petri, an open-source automated auditing framework, to Qwen3-30B-A3B to evaluate deceptive and scheming behaviors across multiple languages. Our findings suggest that scheming scores are inversely correlated with the estimated pretraining language coverage, with low-resource languages averaging 34.2\% higher scores compared to high-resource languages on a five-category scheming index. Furthermore, we find that the effect of estimated pretraining language coverage is not uniform across scheming behaviors.

</details>


### [112] [ProcAgent: An Agentic Framework for Procedural Task Guidance on Edge with Human-in-the-Loop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24770)
*Azizul Zahid,Subrata Biswas,Bashima Islam,Sai Swaminathan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ProcAgent是一个完全在边缘设备（NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin）上运行的视觉程序助手，采用“提议-验证”架构，结合低延迟连续感知、符号任务图、按需视觉语言验证和基于LLM的交互代理，实现实时自适应程序引导。系统仅在有歧义或可能偏差时调用昂贵的视觉推理，支持响应式问答和主动干预（需人工确认）。在感知准确性、推理、任务级性能和用户体验四个维度评估中，纯文本查询约2秒、视觉查询约8秒完成，用户研究（10人）中获得可理解性、可操作性和隐私舒适度的积极评价，证明无需牺牲可用性即可完全在边缘硬件上实现自适应程序辅助。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有程序任务辅助系统多依赖云端推理和固定全时感知，不适用于对隐私敏感、延迟要求高的家庭场景。

Method: 提出“提议-验证”架构：低延迟连续感知持续追踪用户进度，符号任务图维护状态，仅在有歧义或偏差时按需调用昂贵的视觉语言模型验证，并结合LLM驱动的交互代理实现响应式问答和主动干预（需人工确认）。

Result: 完全在单块NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin上运行，文本查询响应约2秒，视觉查询约8秒；10人用户研究中获得积极评价。

Conclusion: 自适应程序辅助可以完全在边缘硬件上实现，且不牺牲可用性。

Abstract: Procedural tasks such as furniture assembly and home repair impose substantial cognitive demands because users must interpret instructions, track task progress, reason about spatial state, and recover from errors while performing physical actions. Prior multimodal assistants have shown promise for procedural guidance, but most rely on cloud inference and fixed always-on perception, making them poorly suited to privacy-sensitive, latency-critical domestic settings.
