We address this with a neuron importance assessment method using three experiments: quantifying overlap between high-weight and accuracy-impacting neurons, analyzing high-weight neuron perturbation effects, and testing post-retraining accuracy after high-weight neuron ablation. Experiments on CIFAR-10 and Mini-ImageNet reveal key patterns. Overlap analysis shows top 10\% high-weight neurons overlap with important ones by only about 25\% at maximum, dropping further in subsequent intervals. Perturbation tests find top 10\% high-weight neurons cause 45-80\% accuracy degradation under certain operations compared to 3-7\% for random perturbations, but a third of them show minimal impact. Ablation-retraining results show removing top 10\% high-weight neurons leaves accuracy 10-20\% below baseline with no recovery, while ablating top 0.1\% allows near-full recovery. Notably, some low-weight intervals show 10-17\% degradation when perturbed, comparable to mid-range high-weight neurons. These results confirm not all high-weight neurons are important: their importance is nonlinear. Low-weight neurons also contribute significantly. This challenges weight-importance equivalence, offering refined neuron role insights. It supports applications like encryption prioritizing critical high-weight neurons and pruning removing non-critical ones, advancing neural network analysis.

</details>


### [180] [Entangled by Design: Spurious Intra-Variable Signal Routing in Tabular In-Context Learners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25532)
*Athanasios Vlontzos,Giorgos Papanastasiou,Bernhard Kainz,Sotirios Tsaftaris*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了上下文学习（ICL）中复合表征的虚假路由问题：当特征X包含因果信号C和设备伪影S时，ICL模型会优先利用与结果相关的伪影S进行预测，导致在新环境下失败。作者证明线性ICL下该路由不可避免，TabPFN表现定性一致，并发现更大上下文会加剧虚假路由（最高1.74倍）。提出两种轻量缓解方法：环境分层上下文构建和S-swap增强，后者可减少74%-98.8%的虚假路由。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ICL模型在跨环境部署时存在严重泛化问题，模型会利用训练数据中与结果存在虚假相关性的特征（如医院设备伪影）而非真正的因果特征进行预测，导致在分布外场景下性能崩溃。亟需理解这一现象的机制并提出有效缓解方法。

Method: 通过理论分析证明线性ICL（岭回归形式）下虚假路由不可避免；在TabPFN上验证定性一致。推导出CSR（因果信号路由比）的闭式解CSR ∝ ρ_S/ρ_C。提出两种缓解方法：环境分层上下文构建（按环境标签划分上下文）和S-swap增强（交换样本间的伪影成分）。

Result: 理论预测与实验高度一致（线性ICL r=0.997，TabPFN r=0.979）。更大上下文将虚假路由放大至1.74倍。高表达力模型在高伪影环境下更脆弱（CSR差距+2.22）。S-swap在TabPFN上减少98.8%虚假路由，同时因果敏感性提升8.4倍。

Conclusion: ICL在复合表征中必然出现虚假路由且无法通过增大上下文消除。所提方法（尤其是S-swap）可有效将模型路由从伪影信号重新导向因果信号，无需因果分区知识，仅需弱环境标签。

Abstract: Consider a model trained at a single hospital to predict patient recovery, where the measured feature $X$ bundles the patient's true health signal ($C$) with a systematic artefact from that hospital's equipment ($S$). Within that hospital, the artefact correlates with outcomes through unmeasured confounders such as patient demographics; an in-context learner rationally routes predictions through $S$, not $C$, and fails silently when deployed at a new hospital with different equipment. We formalise this as \emph{spurious routing in composite representations}: when a feature $X = [C;\,αS;\,η]$ encodes a causal signal $C$ and a spurious signal $S$ in distinct subspaces, the ICL cannot determine which drives predictions. We prove that under ridge ICL, a linear in-context learner, this routing is unavoidable regardless of context size; TabPFN, a state-of-the-art pretrained tabular ICL model, shows qualitatively consistent behaviour empirically. We derive a closed-form characterisation, $\mathrm{CSR} \propto ρ_S/ρ_C$, confirmed at $r = 0.997$ for linear ICL and $r = 0.979$ for TabPFN. Contrary to intuition, larger context sharpens commitment to the dominant in-context signal, amplifying spurious routing by up to $1.74\times$; in the high-spurious corner, more expressive models show greater vulnerability empirically ($+2.22$ CSR gap at high entanglement). We introduce two lightweight mitigations: environment-stratified context construction and S-swap augmentation, that require only weak environment labels and no knowledge of the causal partition. S-swap reduces spurious routing by $74\%$ for linear ICL and $98.8\%$ for TabPFN, with TabPFN's causal sensitivity increasing $8.4\times$ simultaneously: the model does not become agnostic, it reroutes through the causal signal.

</details>


### [181] [From Training to Deployment: Post-Hoc Causal Feature Identification via Sensitivity Ratios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25546)
*Athanasios Vlontzos,Giorgos Papanastasiou,Bernhard Kainz,Sotirios Tsaftaris*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出标准化敏感性比（NSR），一种事后、模型无关的诊断方法，用于在结构化移位（如多中心临床数据）中区分因果特征与虚假特征，通过跨环境下模型敏感性的变异系数实现精确识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法需要访问训练过程，无法在模型训练后（事后）回答模型依赖的是因果特征还是虚假特征，缺乏一种事后、模型无关的诊断工具。

Method: 提出标准化敏感性比（NSR），定义为跨环境敏感性的平方变异系数。在结构化移位假设下（因果机制和边缘分布稳定，虚假特征均值变化），因果特征的敏感性在不同环境下恒定，而虚假特征的敏感性随环境漂移。基于线性结构因果模型（SCM），在K≥3个非退化环境下实现精确识别。

Result: 理论：在满足条件下AUROC=1.000；五种模型族上Kendall τ≥0.529；自行车共享数据上8个因果特征中恢复6个，Precision@7=0.75。完整刻画了失败模式：弱移位（O(ε⁴)坍缩）、退化几何、代理衰减（O((1-α)⁴)）。有限样本率：原假设下O_p(n⁻¹)，备择假设下O_p(n^{-1/2})。

Conclusion: NSR是一种事后、模型无关的有效诊断工具，能够在结构化移位条件下准确区分因果特征与虚假特征，且无需修改已训练模型。

Abstract: Given a model that is already trained, which features does it rely on causally versus spuriously? Existing methods require access to the training procedure and cannot answer this post-hoc. We introduce the \textbf{Normalised Sensitivity Ratio~(NSR)}, a post-hoc, model-agnostic diagnostic for this question under a structured-shift regime: environments differ primarily in the mean of spurious features while the causal mechanism and causal marginals remain stable, as in multi-site clinical data or multi-batch genomics. Within this regime, causal features induce constant model sensitivity across environments while spurious features track shift. NSR formalises this as the squared coefficient of variation of per-environment sensitivity. Under a linear structural causal model (SCM) with $K\ge3$ non-degenerate environments, NSR achieves exact identification (Theorem~1). We fully characterise failure: weak shifts ($O(\varepsilon^4)$ collapse), degenerate geometry, and proxy attenuation ($O((1-α)^4)$), giving practitioners quantitative criteria for assessing whether the regime holds. Finite-sample rates are $O_p(n^{-1})$ under the null and $O_p(n^{-1/2})$ under the alternative. Experiments confirm all theoretical predictions on synthetic data (area under the ROC curve [AUROC] $= 1.000$ under conditions satisfying the regime), show consistent rankings across five model families (Kendall $τ\ge0.529$), and recover six of eight causal features on bike-sharing data (Precision@7 $= 0.75$) without modifying any trained model.

</details>


### [182] [Distilling Temporal Search and Reasoning: Evolving LLMs for Future Prediction via Harness-Assisted Efficient Data Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25554)
*Wanxu Cai,Zhengyu Chen,Huaisheng Zhu,Wei Wang,Jingang Wang,Qiang Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出时间截断框架（Time-Truncation Harness），通过在每一轮强制设定时间截止点，将工具集成推理（TIR）范式应用于历史事件采样，显著降低时间泄露和拒绝采样依赖，提升采样效率。该框架自然诱导更广的时间搜索范围，提高高质量数据比例。蒸馏实验表明，使用该框架干预数据训练的学生模型取得最佳性能，实现了从高质量时序推理数据到参数化进步的有效转化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有未来事件预测方法中，外部智能体框架在移除后预测能力消失；而工具集成推理虽内化了多跳事实检索，但无法处理时序搜索和历史趋势推理。关键障碍是数据中的时间泄露问题——历史查询会将预测退化为检索。先前方法要么使用静态观察冻结信息收集，要么依赖拒绝采样或未解决的新查询，导致大量数据被丢弃，合成效率低下。

Method: 提出时间截断框架（Time-Truncation Harness），在每一轮推理中强制执行时间截止点，使得TIR风格的采样能够应用于历史事件数据，从而减少时间泄露、降低对拒绝采样或未解决查询的依赖，提升采样效率。同时构建大规模语料库和基于过程的评估指标。

Result: 该框架自然诱导了更广的时间搜索范围，提高了高质量数据比例，提升了效率并减少了对复杂评分规则的依赖。蒸馏实验中，使用框架干预数据训练的学生模型取得最佳性能。

Conclusion: 验证了时间截断框架能有效将高质量时序搜索与推理数据转化为学生模型的参数化进步，实现智能体辅助下的模型演化，为未来事件预测提供了更高效的数据合成与训练范式。

Abstract: Future event prediction carries broad social impact yet remains challenging. SOTA approaches augment LLMs with external agent frameworks whose predictive capability vanishes once the harness is removed. While recent Tool-Integrated Reasoning (TIR) internalizes deep search for multi-hop retrieval of facts, forecasting further demands temporal search and reasoning over historical trends and dynamic shifts. The key obstacle is data: historical queries induce temporal leakage that degrades forecasting into retrieval. Prior works either freeze information gathering with static observations, or rely on rejection sampling or unresolved fresh queries that discard vast amounts of data, degrading synthesis efficiency. We propose a time-truncation harness that enforces a temporal cut-off at every turn, enabling TIR-style sampling from historical events, reducing temporal leakage and reliance of rejection sampling or unsolved queries, increasing the sampling efficiency. We further build a large-scale corpus and a process-based metric and show that our harness naturally induces a broader temporal breadth of search and raises the proportion of high-quality data, further increasing the efficiency and reducing the reliance on complex rubrics. Distillation experiments show that students trained on harness-intervened data achieve the best performance, demonstrating harness-assisted model evolving that turns higher quality temporal search and reasoning data into a parametric advancement of the students.

</details>


### [183] [Agent Skills Matter: Inferring Proprietary Skills from Execution Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25560)
*Jianing Geng,Ruiqi He,Zekun Fei,Biao Yi,Ruijie Wang,Zheli Liu,Xia Hu,Xuansheng Wu,Qingkai Zeng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SigLeak框架，证明通过智能体执行轨迹这一行为侧通道，可以重构出专有技能（程序化知识），即使技能本身被隐藏。该框架通过构建诊断性任务、对比有/无技能轨迹、迭代优化，在多个场景下成功重建专有技能，揭示了良性查询轨迹存在技能泄露风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体技能以轻量级、可移植形式部署，可在市场交易或私有化部署，但技能的拥有者倾向于将其保持专有。虽然技能的具体实现被隐藏，但其行为效果在执行轨迹中可被观察，形成了行为侧通道。作者旨在探究能否仅通过良性查询获取的执行轨迹重建专有技能，从而揭示这种安全隐患。

Method: 提出SigLeak黑盒框架：(1) 构建多样化、决策密集的诊断任务；(2) 对比匹配的有技能和无技能轨迹；(3) 从隔离的模式中迭代精炼重构技能。

Result: 在5个场景、3个模型家族、3个智能体框架中，SigLeak在几乎所有设置下优于或持平三种基线。平均将成功率提高6.88个百分点（相对于无技能参考），并在细粒度和粗粒度语义相似度指标SkillSim上取得最高分。

Conclusion: 良性执行轨迹确实可能暴露专有的程序化知识（技能），这对当前智能体技能的市场化部署模式提出了安全警示。

Abstract: Agent skills package reusable procedures that improve downstream performance. Their lightweight, portable form enables marketplace monetization and private deployment behind cloud-hosted agent interfaces, giving providers incentives to keep high-value skills proprietary. Yet hiding the artifacts does not conceal their behavioral effects, which remain observable in execution trajectories and form a behavioral side channel. We define this exposure as Skill Leakage: reconstructing proprietary skills from trajectories elicited by benign queries, without reference answers or success labels. We introduce SigLeak, a black-box framework that exploits recurring skill signatures in agent behavior. It constructs diverse, decision-rich diagnostic tasks, contrasts matched skill-enabled and skill-disabled trajectories, and iteratively refines a reconstructed skill from the isolated patterns. Across five scenarios, three model families, and three agent frameworks, SigLeak outperforms or matches three baselines in nearly every setting. It raises the success rate by 6.88 percentage points over the skill-disabled reference on average and achieves the highest overall SkillSim, our metric for coarse- and fine-grained semantic similarity. These results show that benign execution trajectories can expose proprietary procedural knowledge. The code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/SigLeak-D1DB.

</details>


### [184] [Matrix-Free Photoacoustic Image Reconstruction via Sensor-Token Self-Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25576)
*Mary John,Shibili Said,Imad Barhumi,Sherzod Turaev,Mohamed Yahia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出传感器注意力网络（SAN），一种基于Transformer的架构，将每个传感器的完整时间序列视为一个token，直接从头骨测量数据映射到重建图像，无需在推理时使用系统矩阵。在光声断层扫描（PAT）稀疏视角重建任务中，SAN在SSIM、PSNR、NMSE指标上均优于ISTA、SBTV和LISTA，且推理速度提升至少一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统PAT重建方法（迭代压缩感知求解器或展开式深度网络）在推理时仍依赖系统矩阵，导致实时临床重建计算成本高昂；同时稀疏视角下初始压力分布恢复是一个病态反问题，亟需一种无需系统矩阵参与推理的高效重建方法。

Method: 1）构建解析k空间H矩阵（经k-wave伪谱求解器验证，平均每传感器Pearson相关系数0.919）；2）提出SAN：基于Transformer架构，以每个传感器的完整时间序列为token，直接完成从原始测量到重建图像的端到端映射；3）采用血管加权损失函数，在488个增强样本上训练；4）引入k空间切趾与高斯时间阻尼协同策略，将能量归一化失配降低49%。

Result: 在46个保留样本上，SAN取得最高SSIM(0.522)、PSNR(22.09 dB)和最低NMSE(0.233)；配对t检验和Wilcoxon符号秩检验证实SAN在PSNR、NMSE和Pearson相关系数上显著优于LISTA(p<1e-8)，在所有保真度指标上优于ISTA和SBTV；推理时间减少至少一个数量级。

Conclusion: SAN通过绕过H矩阵的推理计算，在实现高质量PAT重建的同时大幅提升推理速度，具备实时临床重建的潜力。

Abstract: Photoacoustic tomography (PAT) combines the optical absorption contrast of biological tissue with the spatial resolution of ultrasound, yet recovering the initial pressure distribution from sparse-view sensor measurements remains an ill-posed inverse problem. Iterative compressive-sensing solvers and unrolled deep networks both retain a dependence on the system matrix at inference, which leaves real-time clinical reconstruction computationally expensive. This paper proposes the Sensor Attention Network (SAN), a Transformer-based architecture that treats the full time series of each sensor as a token and maps raw measurements directly to the reconstructed image without invoking the system matrix at inference. For training and benchmarking, an analytical k-space H-matrix is constructed and validated against the k-Wave pseudo-spectral solver under matched geometry, achieving a mean per-sensor Pearson correlation of 0.919 +/- 0.049, with k-space apodization and Gaussian temporal damping acting synergistically to reduce the energy-normalized mismatch by 49%. Trained with a vessel-weighted loss on 488 augmented samples and evaluated on 46 held-out samples against ISTA, split-Bregman total variation (SBTV), and learned ISTA (LISTA), SAN attains the highest mean SSIM (0.522) and PSNR (22.09 dB) and the lowest NMSE (0.233). Paired t-tests and Wilcoxon signed-rank tests confirm the superiority of SAN over LISTA on PSNR, NMSE, and Pearson correlation at p < 1e-8, and over ISTA and SBTV on all fidelity metrics. By bypassing the H-matrix at inference, SAN reduces reconstruction time by at least an order of magnitude, supporting real-time PAT reconstruction.

</details>


### [185] [How Small Can You Go? A Controlled Study of LoRA Rank, Target Modules, and Quantization Trade-offs for Text-to-SQL on a 60M-Parameter Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25583)
*Mahendra Singh Rathor,Anagheem Azzam*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过控制变量实验，系统研究了在60M参数的T5-small模型和WikiSQL数据集上，LoRA秩、适配模块和数值精度等效率调节对任务准确率的影响，发现r=16的LoRA在训练不足1%参数、节省31%显存的情况下，准确率仅比全参数微调低11.6个百分点；INT8和NF4量化的QLoRA在极低显存消耗下达到相近准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究大多在数十亿参数的大模型上探索参数高效微调(PEFT)与低比特量化的交互，设计空间探索成本高昂。本文反其道而行之，在一个完全可复现的小模型(60M参数T5-small)和单表Text-to-SQL基准(WikiSQL)上，精准量化每种效率调节手段对任务准确率的具体代价。

Method: 采用控制变量、单因素实验设计，系统研究三个维度：(i) LoRA秩 r ∈ {2, 4, 8, 16, 32}, (ii) 适配模块的选择, (iii) 数值精度。同时报告任务准确率和系统级指标（可训练参数、峰值训练显存、推理延迟、吞吐量），将模型适配视为约束条件下的权衡问题而非仅关注准确率。

Result: LoRA r=16的情况下，训练参数不足全量微调的1%，峰值GPU显存消耗降低31%，准确率（59.6% exact-match）仅比全参数微调（71.2%）低11.6个百分点；秩超过16后无显著准确率增益。INT8和NF4量化的QLoRA准确率分别为52.8%和53.2%，显存消耗仅0.60 GB，展示了极低显存部署场景下的优越权衡。

Conclusion: 在资源受限场景下，PEFT与量化技术可以有效权衡精度与效率；小模型上的受控实验为理解这些技术的真实代价提供了可复现的基准。作者开源了全部代码、配置和日志以确保完全可复现。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) and low-bit quantization are now standard tools for adapting language models under tight compute budgets, yet their interaction is most often studied on billion-parameter models where the design space is expensive to explore. We ask a complementary question: on a specific, fully reproducible 60M-parameter encoder-decoder model (T5-small) and a single-table text-to-SQL benchmark (WikiSQL), how much task accuracy does each efficiency knob actually cost? We run a controlled, single-variable study over (i) LoRA rank r in {2, 4, 8, 16, 32}, (ii) the set of adapted modules, and (iii) numerical precision. We report task accuracy alongside system-level metrics including trainable parameters, peak training memory, inference latency, and throughput, and frame adaptation as a constrained trade-off rather than an accuracy-only objective. Our results show that LoRA with r=16 recovers within 11.6 percentage points of full fine-tuning accuracy (59.6% vs. 71.2% exact-match) while training fewer than 1% of parameters and consuming 31% less peak GPU memory. Within this setting, rank beyond r=16 yields no measurable accuracy gain. QLoRA with INT8 and NF4 quantization achieves comparable accuracy (52.8% and 53.2%) at dramatically lower memory cost (0.60 GB each), demonstrating a compelling trade-off for memory-constrained deployments. All code, configurations, and logs are released for full reproducibility.

</details>


### [186] [A Density-Matrix Framework for Electronic-Structure Analysis of Functional-Group and Salt Effects in Lithium-Metal Electrolytes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25597)
*Mingkang Liu,Huize Yu,Yanbin Gao,Nan Yao,Xiang Chen,Lei Shen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个以密度矩阵为中心的AI平台EMolStudio，用于预测和分析锂金属电解质体系的电子结构。该平台整合了分子功能化、Li⁺第一溶剂化壳层组装、密度矩阵预测及多种电子结构读出的工作流，并应用于163,655个功能化分子和22,500个Li⁺第一溶剂化壳团簇（涵盖四种锂盐）。研究发现：分子尺度上，不同官能团（CO₂Me、CN、F/CF₃、磺酰基）通过π*受体、诱导和极化效应对电子结构产生化学上不同的影响；在溶剂化壳层中，阴离子种类重塑了前线轨道局域化特征（如LiTDI的HOMO始终位于阴离子，LiDFOB则呈现阴离子HOMO与官能团依赖的LUMO配对）。该平台将官能团和盐的选择转化为与锂键形成、去溶剂化和界面反应相关的电子结构假说。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 锂金属电解质的反应性源于分子官能团、Li⁺溶剂化和盐阴离子参与之间的相互作用，这种相互作用通过施主、阴离子和阳离子中心之间的电子密度重新分布来运作。量子化学计算虽然能忠实提供电子结构读出，但在多维设计空间中计算成本高昂，且机器学习电子结构模型很少覆盖化学多样性的溶剂化壳层或电解质相关的读出。因此，需要开发一个高效、可扩展的平台来预测和分析电解质的电子结构。

Method: 提出了EMolStudio平台，其工作流包括：分子功能化、显式Li⁺第一溶剂化壳层组装、带等幂投影的密度矩阵预测，以及前沿轨道、静电势、Li⁺-施主键级和电子局域化的读出。该平台应用于163,655个功能化分子和22,500个显式Li⁺第一溶剂化壳团簇，涵盖四种锂盐（LiTDI、LiDFOB等）。

Result: 分子尺度上，CO₂Me、CN、F/CF₃和磺酰基官能团通过π*受体、诱导和极化效应对前沿能级、静电势和Li⁺-施主接触产生化学上不同的变化，且在高功能化程度下呈现次线性累积。在溶剂化壳层中，阴离子身份重塑了前线轨道局域化：LiTDI的HOMO始终位于阴离子上，而LiDFOB则呈现阴离子HOMO与强烈依赖官能团的LUMO配对的模式。

Conclusion: EMolStudio平台能够将官能团和盐的选择转化为与锂键形成、去溶剂化和界面反应相关的电子结构假说，为锂金属电解质的设计提供了电子结构层面的预测工具。

Abstract: The reactivity of lithium-metal electrolytes arises from the interplay of molecular functional groups, Li$^+$ solvation, and salt-anion participation. This interplay operates through the redistribution of electron density across donor, anion, and cation centers, which is most directly read out from the electronic structure resolved in space. Quantum-chemical calculations deliver such readouts faithfully, yet become computationally demanding across this multidimensional design space, and machine-learning electronic-structure models seldom cover chemically diverse solvation shells or electrolyte-relevant readouts. Here, we present a density-matrix-centered AI platform (EMolStudio) for electronic-structure prediction and analysis. Its workflow integrates molecular functionalization, explicit Li$^+$ first-shell assembly, density-matrix prediction with idempotency projection, and readouts of frontier orbitals, electrostatic potential, Li$^+$-donor bond order, and electron localization. We apply EMolStudio to 163,655 functionalized molecules and 22,500 explicit Li$^+$ first-shell clusters across four lithium salts. We find that 1) at the molecular scale, functionalization distinguishes CO$_2$Me, CN, F/CF$_3$, and sulfonyl groups by chemically distinct changes in frontier levels, electrostatic potential, and Li$^+$-donor contact, consistent with $π^*$-acceptor, inductive, and polarization contributions, with sublinear accumulation at higher degrees of functionalization; 2) in explicit solvation shells, anion identity reshapes frontier-orbital localization: LiTDI anchors the HOMO on the anion across the entire library, whereas LiDFOB pairs an anion-hosted HOMO with strongly functional-group-dependent LUMO hosting. EMolStudio thereby translates functional-group and salt choices into electronic-structure hypotheses relevant to lithium-bond formation, desolvation, and interphase reactions.

</details>


### [187] [Computational Extraction of Legal Causes via al-Sabr wa al-Taqsim: A Set-Theoretic Formalization for Closed Fiqh Chapters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25605)
*Elnaser Abdelwahab*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了al-Sabr wa al-Taqsim（检验与划分）方法的集合论形式化，并设计了一个从法理学裁决真值表中提取最小操作规则的算法，能够计算裁决的最小结构生成元并消除逻辑冗余属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对古典伊斯兰法理学中因果提取方法al-Sabr wa al-Taqsim缺乏严格的形式化表达，需要一种计算化的框架来系统性地从封闭法理章节中提取法律原因（'ilal）。

Method: 采用集合论形式化方法，将古典的检验与划分方法转化为基于真值表的计算算法，通过分析裁决真值表提取最小结构生成元，并消除逻辑冗余属性。

Result: 该算法能够在给定封闭章节的完整真值表条件下，计算出裁决的最小结构生成元，并消除所有逻辑冗余属性，所得结构构成可供后续法理学评估的候选原因。

Conclusion: 该框架的有效性依赖于有限的学派概念词汇表和待研究章节的完整裁决真值表的可用性。

Abstract: This paper presents a set-theoretic formalization of the classical usuli method of al-Sabr wa al-Taqsim (Examination and Division) for extracting legal causes ('ilal) within closed chapters of jurisprudence. A computational algorithm is introduced that extracts minimal operational rules from a truth table of juristic verdicts. The principal result is that, given a complete truth table for a closed chapter, the algorithm computes the minimal structural generators of the ruling and eliminates all logically redundant attributes. The resulting structures constitute admissible candidate causes for subsequent juristic evaluation. The framework is conditional upon the availability of a finite school-relative concept vocabulary and a complete ruling table for the chapter under investigation.

</details>


### [188] [Multi-Sensor Alignment for Weather Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25612)
*Samsad Alam,Devyani Lambhate,Aditya Mohan,Vishal Kumar,Vaibhav Katewa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ReDAM和Unified-weather-edit两种方法，分别用于多传感器间雾的强度对齐和雨雪的粒子位置对齐，以提升自动驾驶感知任务在恶劣天气下的仿真真实性。实验表明，未对齐的仿真会导致3D检测模型性能过于乐观，而对齐后的多传感器仿真数据微调能有效提升模型鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶感知任务需要在恶劣天气下可靠工作，但缺乏真实天气数据集。天气仿真虽是有前景的替代方案，但现有仿真难以保证不同传感器间天气特征（强度、粒子位置）的一致性，导致仿真与真实数据存在偏差。

Method: 提出两种对齐方法：1) ReDAM（参考数据集对齐方法）用于雾的天气强度对齐；2) Unified-weather-edit（受Weather-edit启发）用于雨雪的粒子位置对齐。分别采用统计测试和几何测试验证对齐效果。

Result: 未对齐的仿真版本会使3D检测模型性能过于乐观；使用对齐后的多传感器仿真数据微调现有传感器融合模型，能有效提升3D目标检测任务在恶劣天气下的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 对齐多传感器间的天气仿真特征对于获得真实的仿真数据和训练鲁棒的感知模型至关重要，所提方法在雾、雨、雪天气条件下均有效。

Abstract: Perception tasks for autonomous vehicles need to work satisfactorily in adverse weather conditions. Due to lack of real-world weather datasets, weather simulations are a promising alternative. To ensure simulations closely mirror real-world weather data, it's crucial that they represent the same weather characteristics, including severity and particle positioning, across different sensors. To achieve this, we propose the Reference Dataset Alignment Method (ReDAM) for weather intensity alignment in fog and Unified-weather-edit (inspired by Weather-edit[1]) for particle positioning alignment in rain and snow. We validate both alignment methods using statistical and geometrical tests, respectively. We find that 3D detection models for non-aligned versions tend to be overly optimistic as compared to aligned versions. We also show the aligned-multi-sensor simulation's effectiveness for achieving robustness for 3D object detection task by finetuning existing sensor fusion models on it.

</details>


### [189] [Beyond Epistemia: Epistemic Schizologia and Large Language Models as Techno-Semiotic Machines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25620)
*Federico Cabitza,Gianluca Colombo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文接受Quattrociocchi等人关于LLM导致"Epistemia"（知识体验脱离正当判断实践）的诊断，但挑战其个体主义解释框架。基于Carlo Sini的符号学哲学，将LLM重新理解为"技术-符号机器"，提出"认知分裂症"概念：符号作为语言表达与符号作为社会嵌入的诠释、证据、批判、验证与责任实践之间的断裂。这一断裂由"似真性闭合"（似真延续被呈现为认知结果的终局性）、算法权威和认知自我误认所强化。分析单元应是从提示到使用的完整社会实践，而非孤立模型。据此提出以可审查谱系、可争议性、分布式责任、认知能动性和混合人机实践评估为核心的设计纲领。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战Quattrociocchi等人将"Epistemia"归因于个体认知能力比较的解释框架，指出该框架将认知合法性定位于自主个体的内在能力，忽视了技术与社会实践维度。

Method: 借鉴Carlo Sini关于实践、书写、符号与技术性的哲学，将LLM重新概念化为"技术-符号机器"（techno-semiotic machine），该机器通过从人类书写沉积档案中生成似真语言配置，自动化了书面符号过程的一个阶段。

Result: 提出"认知分裂症"（epistemic schizologia）概念：符号作为语言完成表达与符号作为社会嵌入的解释、证据、批评、验证和责任回路之间的技术-社会断裂。识别出两种强化机制："似真性闭合"（eikotic closure）和"算法权威与认知自我误认"。论证相关分析单元不是模型本身，而是从提示到使用的完整实践。

Conclusion: 重新框架保留了语言生产与负责任理解之间的区分，并建立了以可审查谱系、可争议性、分布式责任、认知能动性和混合人机实践评估为核心的设计纲领。

Abstract: Quattrociocchi and colleagues warn that the fluent outputs of large language models may allow linguistic plausibility to substitute for epistemic evaluation, producing the condition they call *Epistemia*: the experience of possessing knowledge without undertaking the practices through which judgment would ordinarily be warranted. This article accepts that diagnosis but challenges its explanatory framework, which compares an embodied, socially situated human knower with an isolated generative model thereby locating epistemic legitimacy in capacities internal to autonomous agents. Drawing on Carlo Sini's philosophy of practices, writing, signs, and technics, we propose instead to understand a large language model (LLM) as a *techno-semiotic machine* that automates a phase of written semiosis by producing plausible linguistic configurations from the sedimented archive of human writing. From this perspective, *Epistemia* is one consequence of a broader phenomenon that we call *epistemic schizologia*: the socio-technical cleavage between signs as linguistically accomplished expressions and signs as moments within socially embedded circuits of interpretation, evidence, criticism, verification, and responsibility. This cleavage is reinforced by *eikotic closure*, through which a plausible continuation is presented with the finality of an epistemic result, and by algorithmic authority and epistemic self-misrecognition. The relevant unit is therefore not the model alone but the complete practice in which generated inscriptions are prompted, interpreted, verified, contested, used, and made consequential. This reframing preserves the distinction between linguistic production and responsible understanding while grounding a design programme centred on inspectable genealogy, contestability, distributed responsibility, epistemic agency, and the evaluation of hybrid human--AIpractices.

</details>


### [190] [Quotient Dynamics, Effective Curvature, and Implicit Bias in Positive Quadratic Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25624)
*Pengcheng Cheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了正二次网络f_U(x)=x^⊤UU^⊤x的低秩表示结构，揭示了其商流形几何（R^{d×r}_*/O(r)）与训练动力学、曲率、恢复保证及插值偏差之间的内在联系。在因子梯度流与黎曼梯度流等价性、二次回归的有效Hessian刻画、谱初始化下的局部指数收敛、以及欠定通信场景下因子梯度流退化为熵镜像流并选择最小迹解等方面取得了理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 正二次网络的参数化存在商结构（U仅可识别到右正交变换），这种商结构如何影响训练动力学、曲率特性、恢复保证以及插值偏差是核心研究动机。

Method: 利用商流形几何将R^{d×r}_*/O(r)与秩r PSD流形等同；分析欧几里得因子梯度在水平空间的性质；推导二次回归中有效Hessian的解析形式；结合谱初始化和种群曲率分析建立局部收敛保证；在欠定通信情形下建立与熵镜像流的精确等价性。

Result: 因子梯度流精确投影到商黎曼梯度流；有限步梯度下降诱导预测器的精确同余递归；二次回归有效Hessian为经验测量Gram形式在切空间上的限制；谱初始化下梯度流局部指数收敛、小步下降线性收敛；欠定通信中梯度流收敛到Bregman投影，各向同性初始化趋近最小迹解集。

Conclusion: 商结构深刻塑造了正二次网络的学习行为，包括动力学的几何投影性质、插值解的隐式偏差（最小迹选择），且数值实验验证了理论预测。

Abstract: Positive quadratic networks admit the low-rank representation f_U(x)=x^top UU^top x, where Uinmathbb{R}^{dtimes r} is identifiable only up to right orthogonal multiplication, representing a rank-r PSD matrix Q=UU^top. We study how this quotient structure governs training dynamics, curvature, recovery, and interpolation bias. On the full-column-rank stratum, we identify mathbb{R}^{dtimes r}_*/O(r) with the rank-r PSD manifold. For smooth objectives L(U)=ell(UU^top), the Euclidean factor gradient is horizontal. Thus, factor gradient flow projects exactly to quotient Riemannian gradient flow, while finite-step gradient descent induces an exact congruence recursion for the predictor. For quadratic regression, we derive the effective Hessian at interpolators as the empirical measurement Gram form restricted to the tangent space relative to the quotient metric. Under Gaussian rank-one measurements, we compute population curvature, prove uniform deviation bounds for the empirical normal operator, construct a spectral initializer, and establish local exponential convergence for gradient flow and linear convergence for small-step descent. Recovery guarantees are explicit but conservative due to reliance on full-space second-moment control. In underdetermined commuting regimes, factor gradient flow becomes an exact entropy mirror flow in joint spectral coordinates. Strictly positive initializations converge to Bregman projections onto the interpolation set. With isotropic initialization q(0)=varepsilon^2mathbf{1}, predictors approach the minimum-trace solution set as varepsilondownarrow0, resolving nonuniqueness via weighted entropy within the invariant joint spectral algebra. Finite-step descent selects interpolants differing from continuous-time Bregman projections by O(eta). Numerical experiments verify these quotient identities, curvature predictions, recovery behaviors, and selection laws.

</details>


### [191] [Joint Text-Audio Alignment for EEG-to-Text Decoding in Chinese Speech Production and Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25626)
*Tian Zheng,Xurong Xie,Xinxin Zhu,Xiaolan Peng,Feng Tian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EEGAlign是一个参数高效的框架，通过对比学习联合对齐脑电信号与文本语义（BGE-M3）和音频特征（wav2vec 2.0），再经CTC解码进行中文句子分类。在ChineseEEG-2数据上，朗读和被动倾听任务分别达到82.37%和41.43%的Top-1准确率，首次实现非侵入式脑电的大词汇量中文句子解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法仅依赖单一监督轴（文本语义或音频声学特征），无法同时满足大词汇量中文解码所需的句子级判别力和细粒度时间分辨率；且非侵入式脑电信号面临信噪比低、跨被试差异大、输出空间维度高等挑战。

Method: 提出EEGAlign框架：（1）双轴对齐——通过对比学习将EEG分别与BGE-M3文本嵌入和wav2vec 2.0语音特征对齐；（2）CTC字符序列解码；（3）参数高效设计。

Result: 在ChineseEEG-2数据集上，朗读EEG任务Top-1准确率82.37%，被动倾听任务41.43%（101个候选句子）；消融实验表明双轴对齐互补性强，联合使用优于任一单轴。

Conclusion: 首次实现非侵入式脑电信号解码大词汇量中文句子，在较大候选集设置下取得强分类性能，验证了双轴对齐策略的有效性和互补性。

Abstract: Decoding speech information directly from scalp electroencephalography (EEG) into text provides a potential non-invasive neural communication pathway for individuals with severe speech and motor impairments. Compared with invasive approaches such as electrocorticography, EEG is safer and more widely deployable, yet substantially more challenging to decode.This challenge is exacerbated for Chinese sentence decoding, which must handle a high-dimensional output space with thousands of characters, severe inter-subject variability, and low signal-to-noise ratios for text alignment.Existing methods commit to a single supervisory axis---either text semantics or audio acoustic features---yet neither can simultaneously satisfy the demands of sentence-level discriminability and fine-grained temporal resolution required for large-vocabulary Chinese decoding. We introduce EEGAlign, a novel parameter-efficient framework that jointly aligns EEG with two axes---text alignment with BGE-M3 text embeddings and audio alignment with wav2vec~2.0 speech features via contrastive learning followed by CTC character-sequence decoding. On ChineseEEG-2 data, EEGAlign yields state-of-the-art closed-set sentence classification performance, reaching up to 82.37% Top-1 accuracy on Reading Aloud EEG and 41.43% on Passive Listening EEG out of 101 candidates. Ablation studies show that the two alignment axes are highly complementary: combining them yields consistently better performance than either alone. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first study on decoding large-vocabulary Chinese sentences from non-invasive EEG during overt speech production, and achieving strong classification performance with relatively large closed-set candidate-sentence setting.

</details>


### [192] [AIriskEval-edu Demo: Auditing of Pedagogical Risks in Educational Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25634)
*Javier Irigoyen,Roberto Daza,Francisco Jurado,Julian Fierrez,Ruben Tolosana,Alvaro Ortigosa,Miguel Lopez-Duran,Aythami Morales*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AIriskEval-edu Demo是一个评估教学解释质量的平台，使用GPT-5.5和本地微调的Llama 3.1 8B评估器，从五个维度检测教学风险，本地模型在多数指标上优于GPT-5.5，支持AI模式和人工模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 教育领域缺乏系统化的教学解释质量审计工具，现有方法难以同时覆盖准确性、深度、相关性、适切性和偏见等多个维度，且依赖外部API存在数据隐私风险。

Method: 构建包含五个教学风险维度的评估框架，集成GPT-5.5（外部API）和基于Llama 3.1 8B微调的本地评估器（AIriskEval-edu数据集训练），提供二元决策、置信度、自然语言理由和证据定位。

Result: 本地Llama 3.1 8B评估器在多数报告指标上优于GPT-5.5；平台支持两种模式：AI模式（六种预设教师画像）和人工模式（实时审计用户输入）。

Conclusion: 该平台为教育机构提供了一种实用方案，可在自有基础设施内完成教学解释的质量审计，兼顾效果与数据隐私。

Abstract: We present AIriskEval-edu Demo, a platform that audits the pedagogical quality of instructional explanations and provides explainable audit results. The platform evaluates an explanation against a rubric covering five dimensions of pedagogical risk: factual accuracy, depth and completeness, focus and relevance, student-level appropriateness, and ideological bias. For each dimension, it returns a binary decision and a confidence score. Detected risks also include a natural-language rationale and, except for Depth and Completeness, a localized evidence span. The platform integrates GPT-5.5 through an external API and a self-hosted Llama 3.1 8B evaluator that runs on consumer-grade GPUs. The local evaluator is fine-tuned on AIriskEval-edu, a dataset of K-12 instructional explanations with risk and explainability annotations. The platform operates in two modes: in AI mode, both evaluators assess stored explanations generated under six simulated teacher profiles, each representing a distinct pedagogical behavior and potential risk; in human mode, the local evaluator audits user-written explanations in real time. The local evaluator outperforms GPT-5.5 on most reported metrics, offering educational institutions a practical way to keep audited content within their own infrastructure.

</details>


### [193] [Engine-Equal, Human-Unequal: A Reproducible Outcome Skew in Engine-Assessed Equal Chess Positions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25655)
*Jesung Park*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 即便在最强引擎（Stockfish 18）判定为完全平衡（评估值在±10 centipawns内）的象棋开局局面中，人类玩家的实际比赛结果仍然存在系统性偏差：某些局面天然倾向白方，某些倾向黑方。这种偏差在分割不同玩家账号、时间区间、等级分段后均稳定复现（复制斜率0.69，热门局面达0.94），且劣势方思考时间更长。引擎评估不足以充分预测人类对局结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为，在引擎判定为平衡的开局局面下，人类对局结果应由棋手实力决定，而非局面本身。但本研究通过大规模数据分析挑战这一假设，试图证明某些"平衡"局面天然携带对人类玩家的结果偏向。

Method: 收集Lichess上人类实际达到的1,661个引擎评估平衡的开局局面（共1,610万次出现），计算每个局面的结果偏斜（实际结果减去基于棋手等级分预测的结果）。通过三种分割方式验证稳定性：1）不相交玩家账号集；2）时间分割；3）等级分段分割。使用复制斜率衡量偏斜在不同样本间的预测能力，并控制等级分和开局家族效应。

Result: 复制斜率为0.69（聚类95% CI [0.65, 0.74]），最热门局面升至0.94。偏斜中位数|δ|≈0.018（约两个百分点的白方得分率）。该发现通过所有稳健性检验（更紧评估阈值、搜索深度、校准、流行度截断），且在闪电战和快棋中分别复现。劣势方思考时间更长。

Conclusion: 引擎评估即使在最确信时也不足以成为人类对局结果的充分统计量。存在局面固有的结果偏向，其因果机制有待随机对照实验研究。

Abstract: Among chess opening positions that a strong engine judges essentially equal (Stockfish 18 evaluation within 10 centipawns of zero, depth-stable) and that humans actually reach on Lichess (October 2025; 1,661 positions, 16.1M occurrences), human results are not balanced. Positions carry outcome skews, each the gap between its games' actual results and what the players' ratings predict, whose directions are stable properties of the naturally-reached position: some positions favour White, others Black. These skews reproduce across three re-partitions -- disjoint player-account sets (primary), time, and disjoint rating bands -- and on an out-of-sample month eight months later. On the primary split, each position's skew is measured once in each account group, and the replication slope asks how well one measurement predicts the other after removing rating and opening-family effects: one means undiminished carry-over; zero, no linear relation. We find 0.69 (family-clustered 95% CI [0.65, 0.74]), rising to 0.94 on the most-popular, best-measured positions. The slope's value depends on the position mix. Existence is the invariant claim: it survives every tighter evaluation band, search depth, calibration, and popularity cutoff we test, and replicates within blitz and rapid separately. The typical skew is small (median $|δ| \approx 0.018$, about two percentage points of White score), yet it reproduces, position by position, across disjoint accounts. At these positions the disfavoured side also thinks longer. Even where the evaluation is most confident, it is not a sufficient statistic for human outcomes. The result is observational, and the causal question is left to a pre-registered randomised companion study.

</details>


### [194] [OrchBench: Evaluating Multi-Agent Orchestration Plans in Isolation via Deterministic Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25656)
*Zhenzhen Ren,Jiyan He,Xinpeng Zhang,Zhenxing Qian,Ke Han,Shuxin Zheng,GuoBiao Li,Xiaoqing Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OrchBench是一个基于仿真的多智能体编排计划评估基准，通过有向无环图（DAG）对任务依赖关系进行建模，使用确定性模拟器替代真实执行来评估编排计划，仅需1.3%的token和10.3%的时间即可达到与真实执行高度相关的评估结果（Pearson r=0.816）。研究发现保留任务关键信息比增加智能体数量更重要，并行性的收益会随协调失败积累而减弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对多智能体系统（MAS）编排计划的评估通常依赖端到端执行，这种方式将编排计划质量与工作器能力、工具可靠性和环境噪声混为一谈，且随着工作流规模扩大，真实执行的时间和token成本急剧增加，导致系统性评估代价高昂。因此需要一个能隔离评估编排计划本身、高效且可解释的基准。

Method: OrchBench从真实世界任务出发构建有向无环图（DAG）编码任务依赖关系，并控制其规模与并行度。给定DAG、每个智能体的上下文限制和智能体预算，编排规划器将子任务分配给智能体并指定跨智能体信息传输及其保留比率。使用确定性模拟器在不调用工作器的情况下评估执行计划，返回结果质量、完成时间和token成本等可解释指标。

Result: OrchBench生成的模拟分数与Claude Code执行的质量分数高度相关，Pearson相关系数r=0.816，同时仅需1.3%的token和10.3%的实际时间。通过多样化编排器和规模实验发现：保留任务关键信息比增加智能体数量更重要，并且并行性的收益会随协调失败积累而减少。

Conclusion: OrchBench作为一个高效、可解释的基准，能够有效比较和诊断多智能体编排计划，为MAS编排质量评估提供了独立于工作器能力的标准化方法。

Abstract: Complex tasks often decompose into parallelizable yet interdependent subtasks, making orchestration critical to the performance of multi-agent systems (MAS). Existing evaluations typically rely on end-to-end execution, which conflates orchestration-plan quality with worker capabilities, tool reliability, and environmental noise. Moreover, the time and token costs of real execution grow rapidly with workflow scale, making systematic evaluation expensive. We present OrchBench, a simulation-based benchmark for evaluating multi-agent orchestration plans in isolation. Starting from real-world tasks, OrchBench constructs directed acyclic graphs (DAGs) that encode task dependencies, with controlled sizes and degrees of parallelism. Given a DAG, a per-agent context limit, and an agent budget, the evaluated planner assigns subtasks to agents and specifies cross-agent information transfers and their retention ratios. A deterministic simulator evaluates the resulting plan without invoking worker agents and returns interpretable measures of result quality, makespan, and token cost. The simulated scores produced by OrchBench correlate strongly with quality scores from Claude Code executions, achieving a Pearson correlation of \(r=0.816\), while requiring only \(1.3\%\) of the tokens and \(10.3\%\) of the wall-clock time. Across diverse planners and workflow scales, we find that preserving task-critical information is more important than simply increasing the number of agents, and the benefits of parallelism diminish as coordination failures accumulate. These results establish OrchBench as an efficient and interpretable benchmark for comparing and diagnosing multi-agent orchestration plans.

</details>


### [195] [CoRT: Counterfactual Replay for Token-Level Rubric-Guided Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25659)
*Bo-Wen Zhang,Junwei He,Wen Wang,Song-Lin Lv,Wentao Ma,Rongyi Lin,Shuhan Zhong,Lan-Zhe Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CoRT提出了一种基于反事实回放的令牌级信用分配方法，通过对比原始提示和无准则提示下的对数似然差异，为GRPO框架中的每个令牌生成权重，从而在不引入辅助模型的情况下实现细粒度信用分配，平均提升4.4个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在GRPO风格的强化学习管道中，基于评分标准的结构化判断被降级为标量响应级奖励并统一广播给所有生成的令牌，缺乏在响应内部进行信用分配的显式机制，即使不同标准对应不同的文本片段、格式决策或语义选择。

Method: CoRT使用反事实回放，在相同的采样响应下分别计算原始评分标准提示和无准则提示下的令牌级对数似然，将对数似然对比作为对评分标准上下文依赖程度的代理，并将其映射为有界、响应归一化的权重，用于重新分配GRPO优势值到各个令牌，无需辅助评分器或改变响应级奖励。

Result: 跨指令微调模型和奖励粒度的实验表明，CoRT在绝大多数比较中优于匹配的响应级GRPO，平均提升4.4个百分点，且与学习型令牌级信用基线方法保持竞争力，同时避免了单独的相关性学习阶段。

Conclusion: 策略内部的反事实似然对比能够为响应内部信用分配提供有效的训练信号，同时保持GRPO的简洁性和稳定性。

Abstract: Rubric-based reinforcement learning enriches language model training by evaluating model outputs against explicit criteria. Yet in GRPO-style pipelines, these structured judgments are reduced to a scalar response-level reward and converted into a response-level advantage, which is broadcast uniformly to all generated tokens. This leaves no explicit mechanism for allocating credit within a response, even when different criteria are grounded in different spans, formatting decisions, or semantic choices. We propose CoRT, a token-level credit weighting method for rubric-conditioned GRPO. Instead of training an auxiliary token scoring model, CoRT uses counterfactual replay to rescore the same sampled response under the original rubric-conditioned prompt and a matched criteria-free prompt. The resulting tokenwise log-likelihood contrasts serve as a proxy for dependence on the rubric context. CoRT maps these contrasts to bounded, response-normalized weights and uses them to redistribute the signed GRPO advantage across tokens, without introducing an auxiliary scorer or changing the response-level reward. Experiments across instruction-tuned models and reward granularities show that CoRT improves over matched response-level GRPO in the vast majority of comparisons, with an average gain of 4.4 percentage points. The method remains competitive with learned token-level credit baselines while avoiding a separate relevance-learning stage. These results suggest that policy-internal counterfactual likelihood contrasts provide an effective training signal for within-response credit allocation while retaining the simplicity and stability of GRPO.

</details>


### [196] [Localized Adaptation Reveals Distinct Learning Signatures in Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25663)
*Rebecca Ramnauth,Brian Scassellati*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了Transformer模型在不同深度位置进行参数适配（LoRA）对学习效果的影响，发现不同目标任务（如词汇绑定、事实关联、行为学习、因果映射和程序推理）有各自最佳的适配层次分布，即"适应几何"模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管Transformer适配通常分布在整个模型深度，但实际需要改变的范围可能很窄。研究旨在探究适配位置如何影响模型学到的内容、泛化能力以及选择性应用。

Method: 构建了一个包含五种目标（词汇绑定、事实关联、行为策略学习、因果映射、程序推理）的受控基准，定义了每种目标的"适应几何"——即在全栈、早期、中期或晚期层LoRA下的获取、迁移和边界约束特征。

Result: 不同目标呈现不同的适应几何模式：词汇绑定偏好早期层适配；事实关联偏好晚期层；行为学习将晚层行为获取与中层策略门控分离；因果和程序推理迁移最受益于中层或全栈适配。这些模式在参数匹配控制下基本保持，并在五个模型族中复现。

Conclusion: 适配位置是控制模型学什么、如何泛化以及保持什么不变的关键设计变量。

Abstract: Transformer adaptation is typically distributed across model depth, even when the intended change is narrow. We investigate how adaptation site shapes what a model learns, how well that learning generalizes, and how selectively it is applied. We introduce a controlled benchmark spanning five objectives (lexical binding, factual association, behavioral policy learning, causal mapping, and procedural reasoning) and define each objective's "adaptation geometry" as its profile of acquisition, transfer, and boundedness under full-stack and early-, middle-, or late-layer LoRA. The objectives exhibit distinct geometries. Lexical binding favors early-layer adaptation for acquisition and boundedness but requires broader updates for transfer; factual association favors later layers among localized adapters; behavioral learning separates late-layer action acquisition from middle-layer policy gating; and causal and procedural transfer benefit most from middle- or full-stack adaptation. These patterns largely persist under parameter-matched controls, and most corresponding directional contrasts replicate across five model families. These findings establish adaptation site as a key design variable for controlling what models learn, generalize, and leave unchanged.

</details>


### [197] [OmniDelta: Skill-Driven Budget Allocation for Token Compression in OmniLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25669)
*Haoyang Huang,Wenjie Huang,Tianqi Xu,Hongyaoxing Gu,Kang Tan,Yikai Fu,Yuhao Shen,Tianyu Liu,Baolin Zhang,Jun Zhang,Xinyi Hu,Jun Dai,Shuang Ge,Lei Chen,Yue Li,Mingchen Wang,Meng Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出OmniDelta，一种无需训练的、技能驱动的框架，通过意图感知的模态间分配和内容感知的模态内分配，解决OmniLLMs中长音频-视频令牌序列的高内存和推理成本问题，在保持总保留令牌比例的同时重新分配预算，在准确性和效率之间建立了新的帕累托前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有压缩方法主要关注在固定预算下选择重要令牌，但忽略了预算分配问题；直接查询到音频/视频的相似度不可靠，均匀的模态内预算可能遗漏关键证据而保留冗余内容。

Method: OmniDelta包含两个阶段：1) 构建音频和视频技能池，根据查询需求转移固定保留令牌预算，实现意图感知的模态间分配；2) 利用局部复杂度和时间冗余，在音频片段和视频帧之间重新分配模态预算，实现内容感知的模态内分配。

Result: 在四个音视频基准测试上，使用两个Qwen2.5-Omni模型，OmniDelta在多个剪枝比率下建立了新的准确率-效率帕累托前沿。在Qwen2.5-Omni-7B上，25%令牌保留率下，GPU内存减少22.0%，端到端速度提升1.64倍。

Conclusion: OmniDelta通过耦合意图感知的模态间分配和内容感知的模态内分配，无需训练即可有效优化令牌预算分配，可兼容现有剪枝策略，显著提升OmniLLMs的推理效率。

Abstract: Emerging Omni-modal Large Language Models (OmniLLMs) enable unified understanding of text, audio, and video, but their long audio-video token sequences introduce substantial memory and inference costs. Existing compression methods mainly focus on selecting important tokens under fixed budgets, leaving the preceding budget-allocation problem underexplored. We show that direct query-to-audio/video similarity is unreliable for inter-modal budget allocation, and that uniform intra-modal budgets can miss key evidence while retaining redundant content. To address these limitations, we propose OmniDelta, a training-free, skill-driven framework that couples intent-aware inter-modal allocation with content-aware intra-modal allocation. OmniDelta first constructs audio and video skill pools to shift the fixed retained-token budget according to query demand, then reallocates modality budgets over audio segments and video frames using local complexity and temporal redundancy. The resulting local budgets can be combined with existing pruning strategies, preserving the total retained-token ratio while changing where the budget is spent. Experiments on four audio-video benchmarks with two Qwen2.5-Omni models show that OmniDelta establishes a new accuracy-efficiency Pareto frontier across pruning ratios. At 25% token retention on Qwen2.5-Omni-7B, OmniDelta reduces GPU memory by 22.0% and achieves a 1.64x end-to-end speedup over full-token inference.

</details>


### [198] [DecoEvo: Score-Decoupled Co-Evolution of Solver and Rubric-Generator Skills in Text Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25675)
*Jiangwang Chen,Zixin Song,Junlin Liu,Shuaiyu Zhou,Haiyan Wu,Haihan Shi,Chenxi Zhou,Hanqing Li,Xiao Yang,Da Zhu,Guanjun Jiang,Hai Wan,Xibin Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DecoEvo 提出了解耦协同进化方法，将求解器技能和评分标准生成器技能分开优化，通过解耦目标无需使用黄金标准进行优化，在五个基准测试和三个LLM骨干上相比SkillOpt平均提升2.8-5.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本空间优化方法在开放任务中，当求解器在评分标准衡量的维度上改进后，遗漏的维度对优化信号不可见；而简单地进化评分标准也不可靠，因为分数提升可能来自使标准更容易满足。

Method: DecoEvo采用解耦协同进化策略：求解器技能通过标准级反馈更新；评分标准生成器技能通过独立于聚合求解器分数的互补审计（需求覆盖度和响应区分度）进行修订，使生成器更新聚焦于新暴露的求解器弱点。

Result: 在五个基准测试和三个LLM骨干上，DecoEvo在所有官方评估指标下均优于所有对比方法，相比SkillOpt在五个基准平均获得2.8-5.0%的相对提升。

Conclusion: 解耦优化目标可以有效避免评分标准与求解器共同退化的问题，让评分标准生成持续关注求解器尚未满足的新弱点，从而提升开放任务中的优化效果。

Abstract: Text-space optimization adapts large language models (LLMs) by editing external natural-language artifacts rather than model weights, so the optimized artifacts remain inspectable and the model can be treated as a black box. However, most existing text-space methods keep evaluation fixed. On open-ended tasks, this can become a bottleneck: once the solver improves on the criteria a rubric measures, omitted dimensions remain invisible to the optimization signal. Simply evolving the rubric is also unreliable when updates are selected by the current solver's score, because apparent progress can come from making the rubric easier to satisfy. We introduce DecoEvo (Decoupled Co-Evolution), which co-evolves a solver skill and a rubric-generator skill under decoupled objectives without using gold rubrics during optimization. The solver skill is updated using criterion-level feedback, while the rubric-generator skill is revised through complementary audits of requirement coverage and response discrimination that are independent of aggregate solver score. This separation focuses generator updates on newly exposed solver weaknesses, reducing repeated emphasis on criteria the solver already satisfies. Under each benchmark's official evaluation, DecoEvo outperforms all compared methods across five benchmarks and three LLM backbones, yielding 2.8--5.0\% relative gains over SkillOpt in the five-benchmark average.

</details>


### [199] [Cognivia: A Cognitive Behavioral Therapy Copilot for Evidence-Based Mental Healthcare](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25681)
*Qi Chen,Siria Xiyueyao Luo,Jian Wang,Yuan Shi,Haocong Rao,Xuejiao Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Cognivia，一个基于证据的AI心理治疗师，通过整合自动认知扭曲识别和理性回应生成，在权威CBT文本和专家监督下微调轻量级LLM，显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 认知扭曲会加剧负面情绪并导致心理健康障碍，CBT虽有效但受限于专业治疗师短缺。现有LLM方法存在领域特异性不足、回答过于奉承以及缺乏认知扭曲标注等问题。

Method: 基于权威CBT文本和心理健康QA数据构建数据集，采用多阶段提示和结构化生成策略，在行为科学专家监督下微调轻量级LLM获得Cognivia；并提出首个分层质量评估框架。

Result: 在词汇指标、LLM评判（两种互补标准）以及10位行为科学专家的人工评估中，Cognivia在认知扭曲识别和理性回应生成方面持续优于基线方法。

Conclusion: Cognivia有效解决了现有LLM在心理健康应用中的领域特异性不足和回应质量低的问题，为大规模CBT应用提供了可行的AI解决方案。

Abstract: Cognitive distortion amplifies negative emotions and contributes to mental health disorders. Cognitive Behavioral Therapy (CBT) is an effective way to address cognitive distortions, but its large-scale application is limited by the shortage of professional therapists. Although large language models (LLMs) have recently been explored for mental health applications, existing methods still suffer from limited domain specificity, overly flattering responses, and the absence of well-defined annotations for cognitive distortions. This paper proposes Cognivia, an evidence-based artificial intelligence therapist that integrates automatic cognitive distortion identification and rational response generation. Our framework is built on authoritative CBT texts widely regarded as core paradigms and standard references. It is further augmented with mental health question-answer (Q and A) data, and employs multi-stage prompting and structured generation strategies under the supervision of behavioral science experts. Then we fine-tune a lightweight LLM on this augmented CBT dataset to obtain Cognivia. In addition, we propose the first hierarchical quality evaluation framework for assessing LLM-generated rational responses, developed through collaboration between AI researchers and behavioral science experts. Cognivia is evaluated using lexical metrics, LLM-based Judges with two complementary criteria, and human evaluation by 10 behavioral science experts. It consistently outperforms the baseline methods in cognitive distortion recognition and rational response generation, demonstrating its effectiveness. Our code is available at https://github.com/SNOWTEAM2023/Cognivia.

</details>


### [200] [Nudging Sustainable Choices through LLM-Generated Recommendation Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25726)
*Haya Halimeh,Dietmar Jannach,Oliver Müller*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究如何通过推荐系统中的解释（explanations）来推动可持续消费行为。利用生成式AI（LLM）基于助推理论（nudge theory）生成可持续感知的解释，并通过人类评估和LLM-as-a-judge验证。在两个领域（速溶咖啡和酒店预订）的随机实验（N=529）中发现：单纯披露可持续信息不改变用户选择，而行为框架或描述性社会规范显著增加可持续选择并简化决策过程。感知与行为出现分离——用户欣赏可持续披露但不会因此改变选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐系统作为日常消费的中介，具备引导可持续选择的潜力。已有研究证明解释会影响用户对推荐的感知并支持更明智的决策，但解释能否作为一种行为助推（nudge）在决策时刻突出可持续信息，尚缺乏系统研究。本文旨在填补这一空白，探究不同行为框架的可持续信息解释如何影响用户选择与感知。

Method: 1. 利用生成式AI（LLM）基于助推理论生成可持续感知解释；2. 通过人类评估和LLM-as-a-judge审计验证解释质量；3. 在两个领域（低参与度：速溶咖啡；高参与度：酒店预订）进行随机实验（N=529），参与者从偏好匹配的推荐中（附有不同解释）进行选择。

Result: 1. 单纯披露可持续信息不改变用户选择行为；2. 行为框架（framing）或描述性社会规范（descriptive social norm）显著增加可持续选择并简化决策过程；3. 感知与行为出现分离：用户对可持续披露解释评价更高，但并未转化为更多可持续选择行为。

Conclusion: LLM能够以理论为基础规模化生成解释，为基于解释的社会公益干预提供实用途径。本文讨论了生成式AI在自适应解释设计中的启示。

Abstract: Recommender systems mediate everyday consumption, offering a promising channel for encouraging sustainable choices. Prior research shows that explanations influence users' perceptions of recommendations and can support more informed decisions. We argue that explanations can also serve as behavioral nudges by foregrounding sustainability information at the moment of choice. This study investigates how different behavioral framings of sustainability information in recommendation explanations affect user choices and perceptions. Using generative AI, we generate sustainability-aware explanations by drawing on nudge theory and validate them through human evaluation and LLM-as-a-judge audits. Building on this foundation, we conduct two randomized studies ($N = 529$) in a low involvement domain (instant coffee) and a high involvement domain (hotel bookings), in which participants choose among preference matched recommendations accompanied by these explanations. Our results show that, across both domains, merely disclosing sustainability information in explanations does not change choices, whereas framing that information or invoking a descriptive social norm significantly increases sustainable selections and eases decision-making. Notably, perception and behavior diverge, as plain disclosure improves explanation evaluations without translating into more sustainable selection behavior. Our work demonstrates how LLMs can generate theory-grounded explanations at scale, pointing toward practical explanation-based interventions for social good. We conclude by discussing implications for adaptive explanation design with generative AI.

</details>


### [201] [Loss Invariance Determines What Concept Layers Encode: Volume Grounding in Echocardiography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25748)
*Hyunkyung Han,Min Jung Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 概念瓶颈模型在无体积监督时，即使EF预测准确，中间变量（心室容积）的预测值会因目标函数的尺度不变性而坍缩至无物理意义；添加体积监督可恢复尺度，但会轻微增加EF误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有概念瓶颈模型通常仅通过概念预测准确度来验证有效性，但该方法是否充分未知。本文以左心室容积作为概念、射血分数作为最终输出，检验仅凭概念准确度判断模型可靠性的充分性。

Method: 使用视频Transformer编码器，在公开超声心动图数据集上训练。以收缩末期和舒张末期容积作为概念层，EF通过分析公式计算（无残差路径）。比较纯EF目标训练与额外体积监督训练，在1276个保留样本上评估。

Result: 概念瓶颈未增加EF误差（MAE 6.89 vs 7.13）。无体积监督时，预测体积范围坍缩至0.1mL（参考范围35.7/45.7mL），但相关性部分保持。有体积监督时，体积误差从89.8降至25.8mL，EF误差增加0.4。

Conclusion: 概念准确性可能掩盖缺乏物理尺度的概念层。临床模型的可解释中间变量应针对训练目标的不变性结构进行验证，而非仅依据预测准确度。

Abstract: Objective: Concept bottleneck models route prediction through interpretable intermediate variables, and their validity is normally judged by how accurately those variables are predicted. We ask whether that judgement is sufficient, using left ventricular volumes as the concepts underlying ejection fraction estimation from echocardiographic video.
  Methods: A video transformer encoder was trained on a publicly available echocardiography dataset. End-systolic and end-diastolic volumes formed a concept layer from which ejection fraction was computed analytically, with no residual path to the output. We compared training under an ejection fraction objective alone against training with additional supervision of the volumes in millilitres, and evaluated both on 1276 held-out studies.
  Results: The concept bottleneck did not increase ejection fraction error relative to direct regression, at 6.89 against 7.13 mean absolute error. Without volume supervision, however, the spread of predicted volumes collapsed to 0.1 millilitres against reference spreads of 35.7 and 45.7 millilitres, while correlation was partly preserved. We show that this follows from an invariance property of the objective: ejection fraction is a ratio and is unchanged when both volumes are rescaled, so the loss determines the concept layer only up to scale. Supervision in absolute units reduced volume error from 89.8 to 25.8 millilitres at a cost of 0.4 in ejection fraction error.
  Conclusion: Concept accuracy alone can conceal a concept layer that carries no physical scale.
  Significance: Interpretable intermediate variables in clinical models should be validated against the invariance structure of the training objective, not only against prediction accuracy.

</details>


### [202] [Speculate While You Reason: Teaching Agents to Predict Their Next Tool Call via Joint Agent-Speculator RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25816)
*Jiabao Ji,Yujian Liu,Li An,Rohit Jain,Gungor Polatkan,Siyu Zhu,Shiyu Chang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出自推测智能体（self-speculating agent）方法，让同一个LLM模型同时扮演智能体与推测器角色，通过联合强化学习训练，在保持任务成功率的同时显著提升工具调用预测的Hit@1准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型智能体在执行工具调用时存在大量等待延迟，现有推测器（独立草稿模型或缓存轨迹）与部署的智能体行为不一致，导致推测精度低下（推测器-智能体差距）。

Method: 1）将智能体和推测器统一到同一模型中，智能体模式下解决任务，推测器模式下从部分轨迹预测下一个工具调用，并复用前缀KV缓存；2）提出联合智能体-推测器强化学习方法，从智能体自身的rollout中生成推测目标，交替更新智能体和推测器。

Result: 在搜索QA和对话式工具使用任务中，Qwen3-4B的下一工具调用Hit@1从44.1提升至61.2，Qwen3.5-4B从48.9提升至66.3，同时保持智能体任务成功率不变。

Conclusion: 目标智能体本身是强大的下一调用推测器，统一智能体-推测器的自推测方法能有效缩小推测器-智能体差距，兼顾延迟优化与任务性能。

Abstract: Large language model agents often spend substantial wall-clock time waiting for tool call results. Tool-call speculation can hide this latency by predicting and pre-executing an agent's next tool call if the prediction matches the agent's eventual tool call, but existing speculators are typically separate draft models or cached traces that are poorly aligned with the deployed agent's own behavior. We identify this speculator-agent gap and show that the target agent itself is a strong next-call speculator. This points to a simpler design: unifying the agent and speculator within the same model. In this paper, we introduce the self-speculating agent, a single model that both solves tasks in agent mode and predicts its next tool call from partial trajectories in speculator mode, fully reusing prefix KV cache. To enable this dual-mode agent without degrading performance, we propose a joint agent-speculator reinforcement learning method, which derives speculation targets from the agent's own rollouts and alternates agent and speculator updates. Across agentic search QA and conversational tool-use agentic tasks, our method improves average next tool-call Hit@1 from 44.1 to 61.2 for Qwen3-4B and from 48.9 to 66.3 for Qwen3.5-4B, while preserving agent task success.

</details>


### [203] [Distributed Constraint Optimization via Online Learning and Iterative Pricing with Application to Large-Scale Satellite Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25835)
*Itai Zilberstein,Pranav Rajbhandari,Steve Chien,Tuomas Sandholm*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文从两个互补方向解决大规模分布式约束优化问题（DCOPs）：一是将DCOP与势博弈重新建立联系，并采用在线学习算法进行均衡求解；二是提出将DCOP分解为高层元DCOP（任务分配）和底层局部优化（调度）的两层框架，通过迭代定价方法耦合两个层次。在分布式卫星调度任务上，结合两种方法实现了99%以上的观测请求满足率，远超基线方法的87%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多现实世界的DCOP实例规模太大，无法整体求解，需要更高效的分布式决策方法。

Method: 1) 将DCOP与势博弈重新建立联系，采用现代在线学习算法进行均衡求解；2) 提出两层分解框架：高层元DCOP负责任务分配，底层独立局部优化负责调度，通过迭代定价方法更新高层效用函数来耦合两层。

Result: 在分布式卫星调度问题上，结合在线学习与迭代定价框架达到99%以上的观测请求满足率，而最先进基线方法仅为87%。

Conclusion: 提出的在线学习算法与迭代定价分解框架相结合，能够在大规模DCOP上取得接近最优的性能，显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: Distributed constraint optimization problems (DCOPs) provide a popular framework for distributed decision making under limited communication, but many real-world instances are too large to solve monolithically. We address this challenge from two complementary directions. We revisit the connection between DCOPs and potential games, and adapt modern online learning algorithms for equilibrium finding to DCOPs. We show that these algorithms are competitive with representative incomplete DCOP algorithms. We then turn to decomposition frameworks for large-scale DCOPs, motivated by large-scale decentralized satellite scheduling. We propose a new framework that separates a DCOP into two interacting subproblems: a high-level meta-DCOP for task allocation, and independent local optimization problems for scheduling. To couple the two levels, we develop a novel iterative pricing method that updates the meta-level utilities using feedback from the local optimizers. Combining our online learning methods with our iterative pricing framework, we obtain near-optimal performance on real-world decentralized satellite scheduling problem instances, fulfilling over 99% of observation requests compared with 87% for state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [204] [HiSkill: Empowering LLM Agents with Hierarchical Skill Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25853)
*Yu Hao,Jinxuan Cai,Qi Zhang,Yawen Li,Zhiqiang Zhang,Chuan Shi,Cheng Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HiSkill提出了一种层次化技能图框架，通过构建包含技能节点、原子操作节点及有向边的有向图，桥接高层技能与可执行动作，在交互式任务中显著优于现有方法并降低推理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轨迹到技能的方法通常将技能以扁平化集合形式独立存储和检索，未充分利用技能间的相互关系，且高层技能与可执行动作之间存在鸿沟。

Method: 提出HiSkill层次化技能图框架，将交互轨迹组织为包含技能节点、AtomicOp节点和多种类型边（分解、时序转换、兼容、支持、恢复关系）的有向图。推理时检索任务相关子图，通过符号任务状态、活跃技能和检索子图指导LLM代理进行技能切换、AtomicOp选择和可执行动作接地。

Result: 在三个交互环境上的实验表明，HiSkill在性能上超越当前最先进基线方法，同时减少了推理时的token消耗。

Conclusion: 通过层次化技能图桥接高层技能与可执行动作接地是有效的，验证了该框架在长时程交互任务中的优势。

Abstract: Skills have become an important abstraction for enabling large language model (LLM) agents to reuse past experience in long-horizon interactive tasks. However, existing trajectory-to-skill methods often produce flat collections of high-level textual skills that are stored and retrieved independently, leaving skill relations underutilized and maintaining a gap between high-level skills and executable actions. In this paper, we propose HiSkill, a hierarchical skill graph framework that organizes interaction trajectories into a directed graph with skill nodes, AtomicOp nodes, and typed edges. Specifically, the graph connects reusable high-level skills with executable action templates, while also capturing decomposition, temporal transition, compatibility, support, and recovery relations among them. At inference time, HiSkill retrieves a compact task-relevant subgraph and performs subgraph-guided task execution, where a symbolic task state, an active skill, and the retrieved subgraph guide the LLM agent to switch skills, select AtomicOps, and ground executable actions iteratively. Experiments on three interactive environments show that HiSkill outperforms state-of-the-art baselines while reducing inference token consumption, demonstrating the effectiveness of bridging high-level skills and executable action grounding through a hierarchical skill graph. Our data and code is available at https://github.com/BUPT-GAMMA/HiSkill.

</details>


### [205] [Runtime Uncertainty Monitoring for LLM-Based Multi-Agent Systems Using Bayesian Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25877)
*Bart Custers,Koorosh Aslansefat*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于大语言模型的多智能体系统框架，通过令牌级对数概率与贝叶斯网络实现精算风险建模中的不确定性量化与传播，在保持基线性能的同时提供工作流稳定性与不确定性传播的额外洞察。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精算工作流属于高风险决策场景，LLM的概率特性与智能体间的依赖关系可能导致不可靠输出，进而引发错误风险评估、不公平定价及监管不合规问题。现有方法缺乏对MAS中不确定性传播的有效处理。

Method: 提出一个中心枢纽下的多智能体框架，各智能体分别负责数据准备、建模、审查和解释任务；创新性地利用令牌级对数概率，通过长度归一化与校准转化为任务级置信度估计，再融入贝叶斯网络进行不确定性传播。

Result: 该框架能够复现基线精算模型的性能表现，同时在提供工作流稳定性分析和运行时不确定性传播方面展现出额外价值。

Conclusion: 基于LLM的MAS结合对数概率校准与贝叶斯网络，可有效支持精算风险建模中的不确定性量化，为高风险决策场景提供更可靠的AI辅助框架。

Abstract: This paper investigates how multi-agent systems (MAS)-based on large language models (LLMs) can support actuarial risk modelling, with a particular focus on uncertainty quantification. Actuarial workflows represent a high-stakes decision-support setting where unreliable outputs may lead to incorrect risk assessment, unfair pricing, and regulatory non-compliance. To address uncertainty introduced by the probabilistic nature of LLMs and dependencies between agents, a multi-agent framework is proposed in which specialised agents perform data preparation, modelling, review, and explanation tasks under a central hub. The main contribution is a novel approach to uncertainty propagation using token-level log-probabilities and a Bayesian Network. Importantly, log probabilities are not treated as direct probabilities of correctness or task success. Instead, length-normalised log-probability summaries are transformed into calibrated task-level confidence estimates before incorporation into the Bayesian Network. Results show that the framework reproduces baseline actuarial performance while providing additional insight into workflow stability and runtime uncertainty propagation.

</details>


### [206] [Distributing Security Controls Through Harness Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25890)
*William Robert Gore*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SHarD，一种基于Pi agent harness的可分发安全控制框架，通过在商业AI编码代理上嵌入OS沙箱、技能扫描和工具限制三类安全控制，以单命令安装方式实现与直接配置同等的安全效果（调整后评分100%），解决了代理安全控制难以规模化分发的痛点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编码代理正被快速采用，但安全风险是组织规模化部署的主要障碍。现有安全控制缺乏系统化分发机制，厂商原生方案带来生态依赖，不适合所有部署场景。因此需要研究是否可以使用现成的安全控制实现商业化AI编码代理的安全加固，并扩展到分布式用户。

Method: 采用分阶段测试方法论，在四种代理配置（两种商业代理带/不带控制、基线harness、安全加固harness）上，使用基于OWASP Agentic Applications Top 10的23项测试套件进行评估。SHarD基于Pi agent harness构建，嵌入三类安全控制：OS沙箱、技能扫描和工具限制，通过单命令安装分发。

Result: SHarD调整后评分达到100%，与最佳安全配置的商业代理持平，所有测试类别无回归。同时发现模型非确定性导致安全结果不一致，以及自主代理行为可能跨越系统边界，而OS沙箱能直接缓解此问题。

Conclusion: 现成的安全控制可以嵌入并分发到商业AI编码代理上，SHarD方案在保持等效安全效能的同时实现了规模化分发。提出了控制harness适配框架的初步特征，并识别了未来研究的第三个研究问题。

Abstract: AI coding agents are being adopted at historic speed, yet security and risk concerns remain the primary barrier to scaling agentic AI across organizations. Existing security controls for coding agents are not systematically distributed to engineering teams, and vendor-native solutions introduce ecosystem dependencies that may not suit every deployment context. This paper investigates whether off-the-shelf security controls can be implemented on commercial AI coding agents and scaled to a distributed user base via a custom agent harness. A phased testing methodology was applied across four agent configurations --- two commercial agents with and without controls, a baseline harness, and a security-hardened harness --- using a 23-test suite derived from the OWASP Top 10 for Agentic Applications. SHarD (Secure Harness Distribution), a distributable harness built on the Pi agent harness, demonstrated that three categories of security controls --- OS sandboxing, skill scanning, and tool restriction --- can be embedded and distributed via a single install command while retaining equivalent efficacy to direct installation on commercial agents. SHarD achieved an adjusted score of 100\%, matching the best securely configured commercial agent, with no regression across any test category. Notable observations include evidence that model non-determinism produces inconsistent security outcomes and that autonomous agent behavior can cross system boundaries in ways that OS sandboxing directly mitigates. Initial characteristics toward a control harness fitness framework are proposed, and a third research question is identified for future investigation.

</details>


### [207] [Messier: A High-Resolution Corpus for Cross-Benchmark Agent Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25891)
*Stefan Krsteski,Charlotte Meyer,Guillaume Allegre,Tony O'Halloran,Alexandre Sallinen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Messier是一个统一的大规模语料库，整合了957,253条记录，覆盖30个基准测试、714个智能体、11,891个任务和74,205个验证器，通过标准化记录和反事实重新评分，揭示了前沿进展的不均衡性以及聚合规则对排名的潜在扭曲，并推导出与Epoch评估能力指数高度相关的领域特定能力量表。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的AI智能体评估面临环境碎片化问题：任务、框架、验证器和评分规则各不相同，已有工作要么专注于狭窄场景、规模有限，要么需要昂贵的重新运行，导致大量实证结果无法直接比较。因此，需要一个统一的、大规模的基础设施来整合和标准化评估记录。

Method: 构建Messier语料库：整合公开基准分数并补充六个代表性不足的专业和科学领域的五智能体运行结果。每条记录按模型、框架、环境、任务、验证器和聚合规则进行标准化，并附带SOC/NAICS职业与行业分类。通过反事实重新评分分析不同聚合规则的影响，并基于标准化记录推导领域特定能力量表。

Result: 前沿进展在基准类型上不均衡："函数调用"已饱和，"编程"进步最快，"企业工作流"仍最具挑战性。严格的全通过聚合规则在多验证器任务中会掩盖进步并人为改变智能体排名。从标准化记录推导出的能力量表与Epoch评估能力指数排名高度相关（Spearman ρ=0.81），并可按领域、职业、行动空间或验证器类型进行专门化。

Conclusion: Messier为智能体能力缩放、基准审计和评估失败的细粒度分析提供了可复用的基础性基础设施。

Abstract: Evaluating AI agents in interactive environments is hindered by fragmented tasks, scaffolds, verifiers, and scoring rules. Existing efforts focus on narrow settings, remain limited in scale, or require costly reruns, leaving much of the empirical record incomparable. We introduce Messier, a unified corpus of 957,253 records that span 30 benchmarks, 714 agents, 11,891 tasks, and 74,205 verifiers. Messier consolidates public benchmark scores and supplements them with five-agent runs across six underrepresented professional and scientific domains, including a recent legal benchmark. Each record is standardized by model, scaffold, environment, task, verifier, and aggregation rule, with SOC/NAICS classifications for occupational and industry analysis. Using this corpus, we show frontier progress is uneven across benchmark types, with "function calling" saturated, "programming" improving the fastest, and "enterprise workflows" remaining the most challenging. Furthermore, counterfactual rescoring shows that strict all-pass aggregation in multi-verifier tasks can obscure progress and artificially alter agent rankings. From these standardized records, we derive capability scales that align with Epoch's Evaluation Capability Index rankings at Spearman \r{ho} = 0.81 and can be specialized by domain, occupation, action space, or verifier type. Messier provides a foundational, reusable infrastructure for agent capability scaling, benchmark auditing, and fine-grained analysis of evaluation failures.

</details>


### [208] [Interactive Reward Agent: GUI Task Evaluation via Environment-State Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25904)
*Chenrui Shi,Yuwei Wu,Yang Liu,Ruining Feng,Zirui Shang,Zhi Gao,Lifeng Fan,Che Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于"提出-验证"框架的交互式奖励智能体（IRA），通过在任务执行后交互式地获取和验证环境状态证据（包括系统配置、文件数据等），实现了更可靠的GUI任务评估。IRA在GUI-RewardBench基准上达到86.9%的准确率，并成功用于强化学习训练，将GUI代理的OSWorld成功率提升至34.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的GUI任务评估方法主要依赖执行轨迹截图，无法获取环境状态（如系统配置、文件数据、应用设置等），导致评估不可靠。需要一种能够访问和验证执行后环境状态的方法来提升评估准确性。

Method: 提出交互式奖励智能体（IRA），基于"提出-验证"框架：首先根据任务指令提出任务完成条件，然后通过调用系统工具、应用工具和GUI工具从执行后环境中获取证据进行验证，综合可见界面和环境状态信息进行判断。

Result: 构建了GUI-RewardBench基准（含321个GUI任务轨迹，涵盖10个Ubuntu桌面应用类别），IRA在该基准上达到86.9%的准确率，优于现有评估基线。将IRA应用于GUI代理的强化学习训练后，OSWorld成功率提升至34.0%。

Conclusion: IRA通过交互式获取和验证环境状态证据，有效解决了GUI任务评估中仅依赖截图不可靠的问题，且能为GUI代理训练提供有效的奖励信号。

Abstract: Graphical user interface task evaluation aims to determine whether a GUI agent has successfully completed a user instruction. Automated GUI task evaluation has received increasing attention because the evaluation results can serve as reward signals for both test-time scaling and post-training. However, reliable GUI task evaluation remains challenging because the judgments often require access to environment states, such as system configurations, file data, and application settings, beyond the screenshots of execution trajectories. In this paper, we propose an interactive reward agent (IRA) based on a propose-then-verify framework to acquire and verify evidence from the post-execution environment. Given a task instruction and a GUI environment after the GUI agent execution, IRA first proposes the task completion conditions and then verifies them by invoking system tools, application tools, and GUI tools. This design combines evidence from both visible interfaces and the environment state in an interactive process. We further introduce GUI-RewardBench, a benchmark of 321 GUI task trajectories spanning 10 Ubuntu desktop application categories. Experiments show that IRA achieves 86.9% accuracy on GUI-RewardBench, outperforming existing evaluator baselines. We further apply IRA to reinforcement learning of GUI agents, achieving a 34.0% OSWorld success rate, which demonstrates that IRA can provide effective reward signals for training GUI agents.

</details>


### [209] [Toward Standardized Cross-Vendor Agent Tool Trust Management in Autonomous Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25914)
*Ravi Kant Sharma,Ashutosh Uttam,Ajay Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AgentToolMO，一个基于3GPP NRM的信息模型，用于多厂商自治网络中AI代理工具跨厂商信任管理。该模型通过形式化信任状态机、阻尼级联传播、跨厂商通知机制和依赖图回溯评估，实现了信任退化的近实时感知与抑制，仿真验证表明可将故障影响半径从小时级降至近实时级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自治网络Level 4-5要求AI代理跨厂商调用工具而不需要人工干预，但现有管理标准缺乏跨厂商信任可见性的标准化机制。当厂商B的工具被攻陷时，厂商A的代理仍会继续调用它，导致级联服务中断。

Method: 提出AgentToolMO 3GPP NRM信息模型，包含四个核心组件：1）形式化定义的信任状态机，具有可证明的渐进式实施策略；2）阻尼级联传播机制，保证有界收敛；3）通过现有Management Services (MnS)接口实现跨厂商信任通知；4）通过NRM依赖图遍历实现回溯影响评估。

Result: 仿真评估表明，标准化跨厂商通知机制将故障影响半径从未检测传播的小时级降低到受MnS通知传递延迟限制的近实时级。级联传播保证在有界迭代内收敛，通知扩展规模跨厂商域呈亚线性增长。

Conclusion: 该框架在现有3GPP管理基础设施内运行，利用现有协议，为可信的多厂商自治网络管理提供了标准化路径。

Abstract: Autonomous Network Levels 4-5 require AI agents to invoke tools across vendor boundaries without human oversight, yet existing management standards lack a standardized mechanism for cross-vendor trust visibility. When a tool from Vendor B is compromised, agents from Vendor A continue invoking it -- unaware of the trust degradation -- causing cascading service impact. We present AgentToolMO, a proposed 3GPP NRM information model for agent tool trust management. The model comprises: a formally defined trust state machine with provable graduated enforcement, damped cascade propagation with bounded convergence, cross-vendor trust notifications via existing Management Services (MnS) interfaces, and retroactive impact assessment through NRM dependency graph traversal. Simulation-based evaluation across multi-vendor topologies shows that standardized cross-vendor notifications reduce blast radius from hours-scale undetected propagation to near-real-time containment bounded by MnS notification delivery, with cascade convergence guaranteed in bounded iterations and sub-linear notification scaling across vendor domains. The framework operates within existing 3GPP management infrastructure, leverages existing protocols, and provides a standardization pathway for trustworthy multi-vendor autonomous network management.

</details>


### [210] [Penelope: Localized Latent Recurrence for Efficient Structured Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25915)
*Yutong Chen,Shouqian Shi,Xinran Liu,Haochen Wang,Jiaying Wang,Tianxing Xu,Yuanxi Wang,Zirui Ding*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Penelope是一种高效的潜在推理框架，通过在选定的解码器区间内定位循环计算，利用时间调制GRU动态和循环读出状态进行潜在空间推理，并通过渐进式CoT到潜在空间的课程学习，在减少推理延迟的同时保持竞争性精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前语言模型处理复杂结构化推理任务时，要么增加参数规模（导致训练部署成本高），要么序列化中间步骤为CoT tokens（将推理计算与自回归输出长度绑定），缺乏高效的可扩展推理计算分配方式。

Method: 提出Penelope框架：1) 将循环计算定位到选定的解码器区间，下部分解码器一次性评估构建问题条件化边界记忆；2) 通过时间调制GRU动力学和循环读出状态迭代精炼；3) 采用渐进式CoT到潜在空间的课程学习，将可见推理转移到内部循环路径中。

Result: 在开源结构化推理基准上，Penelope在验证选择的潜在预算下，与已有潜在推理模型相比达到竞争性精度，同时降低了测量推理延迟。

Conclusion: 潜在精炼可以定位到狭窄的解码器区间，减少了重复的全解码器执行，无需生成长可见推理迹，为解码器-only Transformer模型提供了实用的精度-效率权衡方案。

Abstract: Complex structured reasoning tasks often require additional computation, yet current language models obtain it mainly by increasing parameter scale or by serializing intermediate steps as chain-of-thought (CoT) tokens. The former raises training and deployment costs, while the latter ties reasoning computation to autoregressive output length. We introduce Penelope, an efficient latent-reasoning framework for pretrained decoder-only Transformers that localizes recurrent computation to a selected decoder interval. The lower decoder prefix is evaluated once to construct a problem-conditioned boundary memory, which is then iteratively refined through time-modulated GRU dynamics and recurrent readout states before answer generation. A progressive CoT-to-latent curriculum transfers visible reasoning into this internal recurrent path, allowing additional computation to be allocated in latent space without repeatedly executing the complete decoder or generating a long intermediate trace. Experiments on open-source structured-reasoning benchmarks show that, at validation-selected latent budgets, Penelope attains competitive accuracy relative to established latent-reasoning models while reducing measured inference latency. These results show that latent refinement can be localized to a narrow decoder interval, reducing repeated full-decoder execution without generating a long visible reasoning trace and providing a practical accuracy-efficiency tradeoff for decoder-only Transformer models.

</details>


### [211] [dtControl2+$\varepsilon$: Trading Optimality for Explainability in MDPs via Decision Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25925)
*Tereza Kinská,Jan Křetínský,Tobias Meggendorfer,Sabine Rieder,Maximilian Weininger*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文扩展了dtControl2工具，通过引入ε-近似最优性概念，允许在给定精度损失阈值ε≥0的情况下构建更小的决策树控制器，从而在保证ε-最优性的前提下大幅简化策略表示，生成的决策树比现有技术小数个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于决策树的控制器表示方法（如dtControl2）在处理大规模系统或存在大量边缘情况的系统时，生成的决策树过于复杂，难以被人类理解。直接缩小决策树又可能导致遗漏关键情形，从而产生不正确的控制器。因此，如何在保证控制器正确性的前提下，有效简化决策树表示是一个亟待解决的问题。

Method: 在马尔可夫决策过程的设定下，扩展dtControl2工具，引入ε-功能：给定允许的不精确度ε≥0，在保证ε-最优性的前提下，通过蒸馏控制器的核心本质，构建更小的决策树。

Result: 所构建的决策树在规模上比现有技术（state of the art）小数个数量级。

Conclusion: 通过可控的精度损失（ε），可以在保证ε-最优性的同时，获得显著简化的、可调节解释粒度的决策树表示，从而提升控制器的可解释性。

Abstract: Over the past decade, decision trees have been used to represent controllers (a.k.a. policies) in an explainable way, with dtControl2 as a current state-of-the-art tool. However, for systems that are large or have many corner cases, even such representations tend to be too complex and not human-comprehensible. Unfortunately, reducing the size of the decision tree is not straightforward, as missing just a single crucial case might result in an incorrect controller. We tackle this issue in the setting of Markov decision processes, extending dtControl2 by "$\varepsilon$" functionality: Given an allowed imprecision $\varepsilon \geq 0$, we construct a smaller decision tree, distilling the essence of the controller, while still guaranteeing its $\varepsilon$-optimality. This enables us to provide tunably simpler explanations, omitting a controllable amount of detail. Our tool constructs decision trees that are orders of magnitude smaller than the state of the art.

</details>


### [212] [A Cost-Effective Multimodal LLM Reasoning Framework for Question Answering over Irregular Clinical Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25947)
*Frank Nie,Ethan B Liu,Yuan Zhu,Wei Fan,Jindong Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ClinPRISM是一个针对不规则临床时间序列（ICTS）的多模态大语言模型推理框架，通过不规则感知多尺度编码器、时间证据蒸馏器和渐进对齐策略，仅用16个时间序列令牌和0.15秒推理延迟，在4B参数LLM骨干上实现了SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型在处理临床时间序列时，无法有效建模数据的稀疏性、异步性和不规则采样模式，亟需一种能适应ICTS特性的推理框架。

Method: 1) 不规则感知多尺度编码器：在不同时间尺度上捕获稀疏临床证据；2) 时间证据蒸馏器：整合多尺度表征并压缩为少量LLM兼容令牌；3) 渐进对齐策略：分阶段将不规则轨迹与LLM文本嵌入空间对齐。同时构建了30,000对临床时间序列与多尺度描述、41,000条覆盖11项任务的指令微调数据。

Result: 基于4B参数LLM骨干，ClinPRISM在保留测试集上达到SOTA性能，仅使用16个时间序列令牌，平均推理延迟为0.15秒/问题。

Conclusion: ClinPRISM为不规则临床时间序列上的高效问答提供了一种低成本、高性能的多模态LLM推理方案。

Abstract: Question answering (QA) over irregular clinical time series (ICTS) plays a pivotal role in a wide range of healthcare applications. Although recent multimodal time-series large language models (LLMs) have shown considerable promise in general-purpose time-series QA, they remain poorly equipped to model the sparsity, asynchrony, and irregular sampling patterns of clinical observations. To fill this gap, we propose ClinPRISM, a cost-effective multimodal LLM reasoning framework for question answering over ICTS data. First, we devise an irregularity-aware multi-scale encoder to capture sparse clinical evidence at diverse temporal scales. Then, we propose a temporal evidence distiller to integrate representations across these scales and compress them into a small number of LLM-compatible tokens. Moreover, we introduce a progressive alignment strategy that sequentially aligns the irregular trajectories with the LLM's textual embedding space. To facilitate training, we construct 30,000 clinical time series paired with multi-scale descriptions, together with 41,000 instruction-tuning instances spanning 11 tasks. Using a 4-billion-parameter LLM backbone, ClinPRISM achieves state-of-the-art performance on the held-out evaluation benchmark while using only 16 time-series tokens and achieving an average inference latency of 0.15 seconds per question.

</details>


### [213] [CHARM: A Multimodal Graph Foundation Model with Hierarchical Context Modeling for Zero-Shot Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26023)
*Ankang Yang,Jitao Zhao,Di Jin,Yuxiao Huang,Dongxiao He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CHARM是一种多模态图基础模型，通过层次化上下文建模实现零样本迁移，将节点替换为多模态图上下文以捕获跨模态关系，并使用感知模态的图上下文编码器和大语言模型生成图令牌，在零样本多模态图任务上取得一致改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界多模态图难以标注且跨域迁移成本高，现有图基础模型需要领域微调，LLM方法主要处理单模态图，零样本迁移在多模态图上尚未充分探索。

Method: 提出CHARM模型，用层次化图上下文替代原始节点，通过模态感知的图上下文编码器整合多模态信息与图结构，将表示转化为图令牌输入大语言模型，实现跨域共享概念映射。

Result: 在零样本多模态图任务上取得一致改进，有效减少对目标域监督或适配的依赖。
